  the password is its own row read from `public.has_password()`, never inferred from
  an `email` identity row. R3 password removal is owner-initiated as well as
  unlink-driven — `public.remove_own_password()` refuses when it is the last way in.
  R4 the reset request is NEUTRAL-ALWAYS (B-3 class): registered, unregistered and
  transport-error all render one identical confirmation, and it never reveals whether
  an account has a password.

- **S.. (2026-08-03):** P1-g Step A — production RLS/ACL deny re-proof, executed
  against ethio-prod. Signed out: `countries` readable (intended public reference),
  `profiles` and `user_directory` refused 401/42501 on read AND insert, and both
  `has_password` and `remove_own_password` refused "permission denied for function".
  Signed in as user A (throwaway pair, deleted after): A reads its own profile,
  reads 0 rows of B's profile and 0 of B's directory, cannot update or delete any
  profile row (42501 — no UPDATE/DELETE grant on the table at all), and
  `remove_own_password()` is refused as A's last method. The full-surface answer:
  personal data is owner-read-only through PostgREST and mutation happens only
  through the audited SECURITY DEFINER functions.

- **S.. (2026-08-03):** P1-g Step B/D — the dependency audit becomes an enforcing CI
  job (`dependency-audit`), failing on high/critical. It distinguishes three states
  rather than two: clean, findings, and *advisory service unreachable* — the last
  fails with its own message so a broken registry call can never be read as a clean
  bill of health (law F4). `bun audit` cannot complete from the build sandbox
  (HTTP 404), so the authoritative result is the CI job's, not a local run's.

- **S.. (2026-08-04):** Audit remediation, probe retirement, fixture refresh (Tier B).
  (1) The 8 high audit findings are cleared by same-major `overrides` on
  `brace-expansion`, `postcss` and `js-yaml` — all dev/build-chain packages with zero
  runtime exposure (INC-025). The `brace-expansion` floor stays inside 1.x on purpose:
  a flat `>=1.1.17` pulls the whole tree to 5.x and breaks `minimatch@3`, i.e. eslint.
  The authoritative clean verdict is the CI `dependency-audit` job's; the sandbox 404s.
  (2) The recovery-identity probe is RETIRED — `.github/workflows/p1g-probe.yml` and
  `scripts/deny-tests/p1g-recovery-identity.ts` deleted. Its census question is
  answered and recorded (GoTrue sets a password without re-creating the `email`
  identity); under ruling R2's truth model that state is legitimate and visible, and
  the invariant is now continuously guarded by E2E R-2 and S-4 rather than by a manual
  dispatch. The `E2E_SUPABASE_DB_URL` repository secret was single-purpose to that
  probe and is now dead — operator deletion item.
  (3) Guard Proof fixtures re-checked against the moved auth surface:
  `c4-arbitrary-recipient.patch` applies clean unchanged; `b3-enumeration.patch` was
  regenerated (the sign-in error block moved ~102 lines during P1-g), same mutation
  intent, forbidden-on-main header kept.

- **S.. (2026-08-04): PHASE 1 (IDENTITY) CLOSED.** Gate evidence: RLS/ACL deny
  re-proof executed against prod (signed-out refusals, cross-user isolation,
  function ACLs); dependency-audit CI gate landed, PROVEN failing on 8 real high
  findings on its debut, then clean after same-major overrides; Guard Proof green
  on regenerated fixtures against the moved auth surface; full E2E suite green
  under the Ethereal sink; recovery flow walked live on production by the operator
  (forgot-password → email → reset → truth-model row restored → new-password
  sign-in). Phase 1 delivered: identity schema with partition seams and honest
  country provenance (INC-022), i18n runtime, email door with resend hardening
  (INC-005/017), Google door with proven REQ-015 linking semantics (D-8/D-10),
  settings surface on the truth model (R2), password recovery (INC-025), and the
  guard infrastructure that caught its own defects: CI status reporter, nightly
  real-time job, guard-proof A/B harness, audit gate. Incidents INC-011..INC-025
  all resolved or deferred-named; supervisor deviations D-006..D-014 logged.
  Telegram + device-list remain in the DEC-012 Additional-auth-doors phase.
  NEXT: Phase 2 (marketplace core) — spec work opens in a new thread per the
  pipelined model.

- **S.. (2026-08-04): Recovery resubmit throttle + nightly sink reconciliation (INC-026).**
  The forgot-password submit now reuses the sign-up resend's single cooldown timer and
  per-visit counter (`RESEND_COOLDOWN_SECONDS`, `MAX_RESENDS_PER_VISIT`) — no second
  timer system — engaged on INITIATION per the INC-017 ruling, behind the same
  synchronous in-flight ref so a double-click cannot start two cooldowns. Past the cap
  the control stays disabled under a neutral `auth.resetLimit` message (new key, EN+AM).
  The neutral-always response (ruling R4, guard B-3) is untouched: identical copy for
  existing, unknown and error. New E2E R-4 asserts the disabled state and the cooldown
  affordance after one submit, mobile-360, no real-clock wait and no mail send (the
  probe address is unregistered, so GoTrue issues nothing). The nightly E2E job's only
  divergence from the green main E2E job was the sink flag: it hard-coded
  `E2E_EMAIL_SINK: "1"` where ci.yml reads `vars.E2E_EMAIL_SINK`; every other env value
  (staging URL, publishable key, service-role secret, VITE_*/SUPABASE_* build vars) was
  already identical, and the workflow sets no SMTP of its own. The duplicated literal is
  what let the Mailtrap → Ethereal swap (R1) pass the nightly by. Reconciled to the
  shared variable; next scheduled run is the verification.

- **S.. (2026-08-04): Nightly heartbeat made rebase-safe and non-masking (INC-027).**
  Two defects in the bookkeeping, none in the tests. (1) The heartbeat pushed without
  fetching, so a commit landing on main mid-run rejected it; (2) that rejection failed
  the job, conflating bookkeeping with the test verdict — and left the status file
  stale at the pre-fix run, which is exactly the staleness the file exists to expose.
  Restructured: "Run nightly E2E" carries `continue-on-error`, the heartbeat runs with
  `if: always()`, and a final step re-raises the captured test outcome, so the job
  fails iff the suite failed and a red suite is never hidden. The push now regenerates
  rather than merges: fetch origin main, `reset --hard`, re-write the file from this
  run's own data (one shell function, one source, used on every attempt), commit,
  push — up to 3 attempts with a short sleep. The status file is derived state, so
  rewriting on top of a moved ref is always correct and cannot conflict. Three
  failures emit `::warning::heartbeat push failed after retries` and exit 0; the
  ~48h staleness rule remains the backstop. Logged against the REQ-032 ops-invariant
  family as the second "watchdog ignores a moved ref" occurrence.


- **S.. (2026-08-04): PHASE 2 OPENED. P2-a Geography built (Tier A).** `public.locations`
  tree per the frozen GEO pre-decision: one canonical table (country → region → city via
  self-referencing `parent_id`), active-only RLS mirroring `countries`, partial index on
  `is_active` plus `parent_id`/`country_code` indexes, shallow ET+US seed. Rulings applied:
  shared reference data (no partition seam — a diaspora user must read the active country's
  locations), shallow seed (the "add my city" path covers gaps, so a missing city never
  blocks a post), names as `name_en`/`name_am` pending the admin translation dashboard.
  The comprehensive world list stays an admin-side static asset, never a DB table. Listings
  will FK `locations.id` regardless of activation state. Build order for Phase 2: geography →
  categories+attribute-builder → listings+lifecycle → screening gateway (seam-first, filled
  here) → feed/home → search → storefronts → messaging. Seam-first screening ruled by
  operator (the REQ-021 gateway lands at P2-d; earlier features route writes through a
  bypass-proof seam). Census note: no `update_updated_at_column()` helper existed in the
  database — the first migration attempt failed on it (42883) and the retry creates it
  idempotently before the trigger.

D-015 — The supervisor initially leaned to a translations TABLE for location names;
census showed no such table exists and i18n is static locale files, so the design was
corrected to the live `name_en` pattern already used by `public.countries`. Decided
under G17. Logged, not absorbed.

- **S.. (2026-08-04): P2-b Categories + attribute schema built (Tier A).** Three tables
  per REQ-017's three-concept model: `public.categories` (the canonical node — one row
  per real category; a listing FKs it and lives in exactly one category in v1, carrying
  `price_enabled` per REQ-018, `expiry_days` per REQ-022, and the `is_restricted`
  screening seam per REQ-009/010), `public.category_tree_pointers` (the browse tree as
  POINTERS not copies, so one canonical category appears under several parents — one
  inventory, many paths, split-inventory impossible by construction), and
  `public.category_attributes` (REQ-020 structured fields, with a CHECK making `options`
  presence exactly equivalent to a select type). Deny-by-default RLS mirroring
  `locations`: active-only public read on `categories`, `USING (true)` on the two
  dependent tables (they inherit visibility from their gated category — restating the
  predicate would force a join per read for no confidentiality gain), NO write policy on
  any of the three, SELECT-only grants to `anon`/`authenticated`. Starter seed of 12 real
  ethio.com top-levels, each with a top-level browse root, plus one illustrative Vehicles
  attribute set (make/model/year/transmission/condition). Rulings applied:
  starter-seed-now (the authoritative WooCommerce import with dedupe/repair and
  empty-depth collapsing is a separate later task), attribute-builder ADMIN UI deferred
  to the admin console (this phase seeds attributes via migration), names as
  `name_en`/`name_am` on the same basis as D-015. Not seeded per REQ-017 rulings:
  prescription pharmaceuticals (v1 exclusion), Jobs & Vacancies + Tenders (v2).
  Collections (REQ-017 concept 3) are NOT built this phase. Read-back on `ethio-prod`:
  12 categories, 12 pointers, 5 attributes.

  P2-a staging parity: **NOT verified.** Prod carries P2-a exactly as specified
  (verified 2026-08-04); the staging read-back was never obtained — this sandbox holds
  only the prod service-role binding. Recorded as UNPROVEN, not "no drift", and carried
  as an operator checklist item alongside the P2-b staging application.

  INC-028 (duplicate `public.update_updated_at_column()` entry observed in `pg_proc`)
  remains queued for the P2 gate; untouched here. The function is callable and both new
  `updated_at` triggers bind to it successfully.

D-016 — Step 4 of the task asked to record "P2-a locations parity verified across prod
+ staging (no drift)". Staging was never read (no staging credentials in this
environment), so the claim was NOT written; the ledger records UNPROVEN instead.
Deviation logged under A3 (honesty before action), not absorbed.

INC-029 (2026-08-04) — Reported staging locations drift investigated and DISMISSED as a
measurement error. Prod `public.locations` = 32 rows (country 2 / region 12 / city 18),
0 duplicate natural keys, all P2-a constraints and indexes present. The "18" in the
report is the city count; staging's 32 equals prod's 32. No cleanup SQL executed and
none needed; no migration written (prod is correct, and a data cleanup would in any case
be staging-only operator SQL, never a committed migration). Root-cause finding on
re-runnability: P2-a's CREATE TABLE and seed INSERTs share one migration file applied in
a single implicit transaction, so a failed re-apply rolls the seed back with the DDL —
duplicate stacking was never mechanically available. Future migrations that must survive
re-application need guarded DDL + `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` seeds, not explicit
transaction wrapping. Staging parity stays UNPROVEN per D-016 until the operator runs the
diagnostic block against ethio-staging.

- **S.. (2026-08-04): P2-c Listings core + lifecycle + screening seam built (Tier A).**
  `public.listings` (REQ-019) — the marketplace's central object and, by its `id`, the
  rateable-interaction anchor (REQ-011); `public.listing_photos` with the `exif_stripped`
  DEC-009 gate. Two `SECURITY DEFINER` seam functions are the ONLY write paths:
  `public.submit_listing(...)` (ownership assert, active category/location validation,
  price-mode and `price_enabled` rules per REQ-018, attribute validation against
  `category_attributes`, `expires_at` computed from `categories.expiry_days` per REQ-022,
  `published_at` on first draft→active, and a MARKED PASS-THROUGH STUB where the REQ-021
  screening gateway lands at P2-d) and `public.transition_listing(id, status)` (the whole
  state machine — draft→active→expired|sold|removed, active→active renewal resets expiry,
  illegal moves refused; §7 anti-state-scatter). `public.expire_stale_listings()` is
  AUTHORED but NOT SCHEDULED — the pg_cron/external wiring is a named follow-up.
  RLS deny-by-default: SELECT-only grants, active-only public read plus seller-own read on
  both tables, no INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE policy anywhere. Private `listing-photos` bucket
  (never public) with path-prefix ownership on `storage.objects` and public read gated on
  `exif_stripped` + active parent. CI job "Listing-write seam guard (with self-test)" runs
  `scripts/check-listing-writes.sh` against `src/` (passes, 0 findings) and against
  `scripts/fixtures/bad-listing-write-example.ts.txt` (fails, 2 findings) — proven in both
  directions. Rulings applied: photos stored-not-surfaced pending the EXIF strip (DEC-009,
  P2-c-photos is the immediate next pass); the seam is enforced by CI guard; the state
  machine exactly as specified. No `src/` change; no existing migration edited.

**Standing rule adopted (2026-08-04): IDEMPOTENT MIGRATIONS.** Every migration from this
one onward must be re-runnable — `CREATE TABLE/INDEX IF NOT EXISTS`, constraints added
inside `DO $$ ... EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object` blocks, `DROP POLICY/FUNCTION IF EXISTS`
before `CREATE`, `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` on all seeds — so applying it to a database that
already carries it is a no-op rather than an abort. Follows directly from the INC-029
re-runnability finding. P2-c is the first migration written under this rule.

D-017 — Attribute validation in `submit_listing` is IMPLEMENTED, not deferred, but at a
documented depth: required-key presence, `number`/`boolean`/`text`/`select` JSON-type
conformance, and `select` membership in the declared `options`. Not covered yet:
cross-field/conditional rules, numeric ranges, and unit coercion — these land with the
attribute builder at P2-d. Logged as a scope note, not a silent gap.

D-018 — The `listing-photos` bucket row was created through the platform's storage-bucket
tool rather than an `INSERT INTO storage.buckets` statement inside the migration (that SQL
path is rejected by the toolchain). The bucket's RLS — the security-bearing part — IS in
the migration. Consequence: a replay of this migration on a fresh database recreates the
policies but not the bucket row; the operator checklist therefore includes creating the
private `listing-photos` bucket on staging before applying.

D-019 — The post-migration security linter reported 8 WARNs. Seven are the pre-existing
"SECURITY DEFINER function is executable" class and are BY DESIGN here: `submit_listing`
and `transition_listing` are definer-by-necessity (they are the write gate over a
deny-by-default table) and are granted to `authenticated` only, revoked from
`anon`/`PUBLIC`; `expire_stale_listings` is `service_role` only. The single anon-executable
definer function is the platform-owned `public.rls_auto_enable` event-trigger helper, not
ours. WARN 8 (leaked-password protection disabled) is a pre-existing Auth console toggle,
carried to the operator checklist. No new finding was introduced by P2-c.

## Session — 2026-08-04 — Design foundation (P2-design)

Built the ethio.com house style, the first substantial frontend build; every later page
inherits from it. Delivered: the unified `AppShell` (header / rail / body / footer slots,
one skeleton at every breakpoint, rail collapsing to a `ui/sheet` drawer below `lg`); the
config-driven, permission-gated panel system (`src/config/panels.ts` + `panels.types.ts`,
`PANELS` / `panelsForUser` / `visibleItems`, Marketplace always first and always present);
the coffee-on-cool-slate palette as oklch values inside the existing `@theme inline`
structure with measured WCAG ratios; Inter + Bricolage Grotesque + Noto Sans Ethiopic with
the per-glyph Ge'ez fallback; dark mode across every token; the tibeb woven-diamond brand
mark and loading spinner (inline SVG, `prefers-reduced-motion` respected); and the
Marketplace feed's SHAPE — ranked grid, listing card, loading/empty/error states.

**Tier is LIVE.** A Tier-A idempotent migration added `public.listings.tier`
(`text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'regular'`, CHECK `premium|featured|regular`, added inside a
`DO $$ ... EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object` block) plus the partial index
`listings_feed_order_idx ON (tier, published_at DESC) WHERE status = 'active'`, which
serves the feed ordering exactly. `tier` is an operator lever: admin → premium, user
self-serve → featured, both free in v1. No backfill needed (default covers all rows; there
are 0 listings). **Operator action: apply this migration to `ethio-staging` too.**

Documented seams (NOT live): `view_count` (no column; view tracking is a separate
pre-launch feature — every listing reports 0 and the ascending-view sort is a live no-op)
and location scope (`ctx.locationScope` accepted, never narrowed; city→region→country→world
widening is a separate pre-launch feature). Both are commented at their definition in
`src/features/feed/ranking.ts`.

Stubs / TODOs recorded explicitly:
- TODO(rbac) — `isAdmin: false`, `permissions: []` in `src/components/app-shell.tsx`; the
  roles/permissions tables are a later feature. Law F3 unaffected: gating is UI-only.
- TODO(search) — the header search input is visual; submit is a deliberate no-op, there is
  no `/search` route yet.
- TODO(footer-links) — About / How it works / Safety / Contact / Terms / Privacy render as
  text; those pages do not exist yet.
- Category tree: **wired live** (reads `public.categories` + `category_tree_pointers`), not
  stubbed. Category filter on the feed: **wired live**.

D-020 — `index.html` does not exist on this stack. Font `<link>` tags were placed in
`src/routes/__root.tsx`'s `head().links` (the only `<head>` composer) instead; a remote
`@import` in `src/styles.css` would break the Lightning CSS build. Deviation from the
task's stated scope path, same intent.

D-021 — Light `--muted-foreground` was darkened from the specified `#7A828C` (3.63:1 on
`#F6F7F9`, below WCAG AA) to `#686F78` (4.74:1). Dark mode `--primary` uses the lighter
leaf `#7FC9A6` with near-black text: the deep `#1E5A43` scores 8.08:1 against white text
but only ~1.5:1 against the `#14181C` page, so filled controls would disappear.

D-022 — The rail is built on `ui/sheet` (the same primitive `ui/sidebar` uses internally
for its mobile mode) plus a plain `<aside>` at `lg`, rather than `SidebarProvider`. The
sidebar primitive's cookie-persisted icon-collapse model is more machinery than the two
states this shell has; no collapse logic was reinvented.

D-023 — The active panel is client state in the shell context, not a route segment: the
non-Marketplace panels have no routes yet. Their bodies render the "coming in its own
feature" placeholder.

D-024 — `e2e/shell.spec.ts` could not be executed through the Playwright harness in the
build sandbox: `e2e/global-setup.ts` hard-refuses to run without
`E2E_SUPABASE_URL`/`E2E_SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` pointing at `ethio-staging`, and those
secrets live in CI only. Every assertion in the spec was instead executed directly against
the running app at both 360px and 1280px and passed (evidence in the completion report).
CI is the authoritative run.

Launch-gate additions: the brand mark is a WORKING logo pending professional trademark
clearance before commercial use. The motif-neutrality rule (religiously neutral tibeb
geometry only, never a cross or faith iconography of any tradition; motif only as logo,
spinner and empty-state mark) is recorded as a STANDING design rule in
`docs/features/panels.md`.

Session 2026-08-04 (design-foundation CI reds): INC-030 gitleaks prose false positive
resolved by single-fingerprint `.gitleaksignore` (rule stays armed). INC-031 records the
blast-radius rule now in force — an always-mounted panel MUST fail soft and visibly
inside its own body; no feature's backend gap may cascade through the shell-wrapped root.

Session 2026-08-04 (post-design-foundation E2E green + mobile/perf/security pass): all
eleven CI reds were stale-selector test debt from before the AppShell (INC-032) plus a
hydration race that made two of them look like app bugs (INC-033); the application was
healthy in both cases. Mobile pass found and fixed one real law-C2 violation (INC-034)
and converted the 360px rules — no horizontal overflow, ≥44px tap targets, no sub-11px
text, rail-is-drawer below `lg` and persistent above, grid reflow — into assertions.
Performance pass: fonts load `display=swap` with Noto Sans Ethiopic subset to `ethiopic`
and Inter to `latin`; listing cards reserve a fixed `aspect-4/3` box so the
loading→empty/error transition costs no CLS; the marketplace module graph (28 modules)
imports no chart/map/3D library, now guarded by `scripts/check-marketplace-imports.mjs`
in CI. Security pass: the feed is read-only (`status = 'active'` filter over the public
RLS policy, no insert/update/delete anywhere in the feed or card modules), the browser
holds only the publishable key, the search input is inert and builds no query, and the
admin panel is UI-gated only — NEW LAUNCH-GATE NOTE: admin route bodies and admin data
reads MUST carry server-side RLS/RBAC before they ship; the `isAdmin` panel flag is
convenience, never authorization (law F3). Nothing admin-scoped is fetched today, so
there is nothing to leak yet.

Session 2026-08-04 (final layout refinement): the shell's presentation is frozen as the
five-band vertical stack (top bar → panel tabs → location row → breadcrumbs → body)
inside the unchanged corner-block grid, with band separation carried by hairlines alone
so band spacing is symmetric by construction. Panel switching moved from a top-bar
dropdown to a tab row that is ABSENT for logged-out, Marketplace-only visitors, plus the
drawer list on mobile. The geographic axis of the marketplace is now VISIBLE but not yet
ENFORCED: the location row cascades the real `public.locations` tree and writes the chosen
node into `useFeed` alongside `categoryId`, while narrowing, IP resolution and the
city→region→country→world widening ladder remain the pre-launch location-scoping feature
(docs/features/location-scoping.md). Category filtering and breadcrumb navigation are
live. No new colour entered the palette.

Session 2026-08-05 (layout correction pass): the responsive law is now ONE breakpoint —
`md` (768px) — for both sidebar collapse and top-bar minimization. Tablets are not
phones: from `md` up the shell shows the persistent rail and FULL controls (search FIELD
with placeholder, language NAME, labelled account/sign-in); below `md` (phones only) the
rail is the drawer and the bar minimizes to icons with the full-width search row beneath.
Verified at 360/768/1280 with zero horizontal overflow. The top bar carries no intrinsic
height at `md`+ and fills grid row 1, so bar and logo cell share top and bottom edges by
construction. The footer is an explicit equal-thirds grid with halved row spacing and
intact 44px tap boxes. The location row displays the resolved area exactly once (the
picker holding it reads as its level name). Home is the breadcrumb root only and means
the unfiltered Marketplace feed — clicking it restores the marketplace panel and clears
the category; the panel tab remains "Marketplace". Presentational only: no new colour, no
auth/settings/feed behaviour change, location filtering still stubbed.

## Layout polish round 2 (2026-08-05)

Home is the breadcrumb ROOT and, on the marketplace panel, the only chrome segment:
`Home › <category path>`. Other panels keep their name (`Home › Account › …`). The feed
body is centred with equal left/right gutters at every width. Exactly one sidebar
affordance exists per breakpoint — hamburger below `md`, collapse toggle at `md`+ — and
collapsing the rail moves the wordmark into the top bar so the chrome is never unbranded
and never shows the wordmark twice. Footer rows are tightened without breaking the 44px
tap floor. Presentational only: no token, auth, feed or location-scoping behaviour change.

Brand-in-chrome rule: exactly one brand lockup renders per rail state — corner cell when
the rail is open, mark-less two-line lockup in the top bar when it is collapsed. Chrome
controls of fixed size are never crowded by flexible ones: the bar search field is capped
per breakpoint. Reference data (category tree) is cached per page session and shows
skeletons, never an empty rail, on first read.

- 2026-08-05 session — Tier B fix pass: collapse-toggle mobile leak fixed at the class level (INC-055); panel-tabs horizontal overflow removed (INC-056); category caching confirmed (INC-057). No new colors, no auth/settings/feed behavior change.

### DEC-013 (2026-08-07) — Phase resequencing: ladder realignment (RBAC-first restored)
Trigger: operator-directed deep plan review. Finding: the A–E posting plan built governance-§4 ladder Phases 3–4 material while skipping Phase 2 (RBAC & panels); phase labels had drifted (system-state "Phase 2 = Marketplace core" vs governance "Phase 2 = RBAC & panels"). Supervisor forward-scan slip logged (S25).
Ruled (operator, ratified 2026-08-07):
1. Phase R (RBAC core) inserts NOW, before A1. DB layer only: roles / permissions / role_permissions / user_roles (with country scope), resources, audit_log, has_permission() as sole authority, is_system DB triggers (deny even superadmin), last-superadmin protection, requires_step_up seam column, seed ~3 system roles + superadmin bootstrap (operator account), retrofit admin-write policies onto all existing tables, deny-case tests, route↔permission CI guard armed, panel routing on real permissions with redirect-not-dead-end (REQ-030). Admin UI stays in Phase C.
2. All tables created after Phase R write their policies against has_permission() at birth.
3. Discovery and Contact become named pre-launch phases (F: real feed + geo-scope backend + search v1 + REQ-004 translation layer; G: messaging + block/report).
4. Phase B additions: per-action rate limit on post (REQ-038's first consumer), ToS/Privacy v1 pages (Step-8 dependency), My Listings management basics (edit/renew/mark-sold).
5. Translation dashboard slots into the admin epoch after C1 (as C3).
6. ToS/Privacy (operator directive): supervisor drafts v1 pages from the REQ-034 source ledger, marked pre-counsel. Professional legal review remains deferred to the ECA-registration / Ethiopia-entity milestone per REQ-035 and gates ONLY that milestone — it never blocks global launch.
7. New requirements adopted: REQ-039 (PWA mechanics), REQ-040 (observability) — see below.
8. Class-2 provider selections (currency rates, analytics, error-monitoring vendor) deferred to their respective specs — operator discussion at each step.
9. Phase-label repair: the marketplace epoch carries letters R, A–G; the governance 0–9 ladder remains the master map.
10. Mechanism: service-role edge function mints a ≤15-minute target JWT carrying impersonator_id; isolated /impersonate context; token in memory only; auth-service operations unreachable. PREREQUISITE: the write-guard census across every user-writable table and RPC. Tracked as ACT-U3-1.

REQ-017 AMENDMENTS (2026-09-02):

 (a) Jobs & Vacancies and Tenders are not marketplace categories; any future jobs offering is a separate product decision with its own spec. The A1-seeded jobs root and children retire in C1 (is_active=false).

 (b) Visibility dimensions: category nodes carry is_active + optional visible_from/visible_until (timestamptz, NULL=always) + per-country exclusion rows (category_country_exclusions denylist). A browse path is visible only when every node on it is visible for the viewer's country and the current time. Posting is server-gated on visibility; listing detail pages are never gated (merchandising, not access control).

 (c) Catch-all law: exactly one "Other <Name>" child (is_catchall=true) per branching node; sorted last; hidden from browse while empty (empty-depth law applied per node); always poster-visible; inherits parent attributes; graduation via console reassign.

 (d) Transaction-type law (L1): offer type (sale/rent/lease/hire) is a required attribute on Real Estate and applicable Vehicle nodes, never a category. Type-collapse attributes replace enumerated live sub-branches per the ratified tree notes.

 (e) The ratified 2-level target tree is adopted; the tree is admin-dynamic and node counts are advisory, not spec constraints.

REQ-019 NOTE (2026-09-02): every category node carries three image variants — thumbnail (derived), card 512 (the photo-less listing fallback), large/OG 1200×630 (composed programmatically from the icon; AI-OG is fallback). One AI generation per node.

REQ-020 NOTE (2026-09-02): brand/make is modeled as a category attribute, never a standalone entity (apex precedent; ruling 2026-09-02).

## S35 — Stabilisation, import/export and dependent options

DEC-036 (amended 2026-09-06) — Single-owner maintenance: the reaper, DEC-036 audit prune, storage sweep and ratified-am tripwire run only in the shard-1 job; every other job skips them (the t=0 thundering herd of nine concurrent setups is retired).
DEC-038 — Identity pool: the harness mints one pre-enrolled super admin per worker slot per job; tests declare `@private-identity` when they mutate their own auth state (impersonation, factor enrol/unenrol); a guard script refuses such tests without the tag.
DEC-039 — API roles' statement_timeout raised 8s → 30s (authenticated, anon) so pool queueing surfaces as latency the test budgets can see, not as 57014.
DEC-040 — Step-up freshness is a token fact: the client derives freshness from the JWT (aal2 + totp amr within the DB's 10-minute window); no client hint; `guard()` resolves when the action completes, including after a step-up round-trip (the SyncKeysCard result-box bug — a production phantom-nothing after a stale step-up — is fixed and regression-tested by TR-7b).
DEC-041 (amended twice) — Session per test, in node: each test signs in its worker's pooled identity by password, verifies TOTP in node, injects that session; no refresh token is shared across tests. The pool always injects; private mints and auth specs always use the real door; the E2E_UI_LOGIN knob is inert pending deletion from ci.yml and nightly-e2e.yml.
DEC-042 (candidate) — Helper/setup changes should run the smoke tier in the fast lane (today the lane runs nothing when no spec changed).
DEC-043 — Posting law for attributes: card attributes (≤3 ranked) are required at posting because they render on the listing card; every other attribute linked to the category or its ancestors is shown and optional; the listing shows filled attributes only.
DEC-044 — Inheritance law: a link applies to the category and every descendant; the console shows inherited rows read-only with their origin; the amber "needs card attributes" flag reads the effective set; the importer refuses inherited-row edits.
DEC-045 — Dependent options: `attributes.depends_on` (single_select parent), option `parent` values validated against the parent's list; cascading pickers/filters; delete/unlink/merge refused while dependents exist; importable with parent-before-dependent ordering and cycle refusal.
DEC-023 (LIVE 2026-09-07) — Lovable holds staging E2E credentials via its secret dialog; `bun run e2e:local` mirrors CI's build and runtime env (VITE_E2E, staging VITE_SUPABASE_*, E2E_UI_LOGIN=1, E2E_FAKE_TRANSLATE=1, workers), regenerates the i18n usage map first, refuses to start on an occupied 4173, and never reuses a server. Every landing touching e2e/** or a gated surface runs its specs locally before committing and pastes the summary; a run blocked by staging parity means no commit. The platform commits when the executor's turn ends — "commit" is never an operator action.
INC-160…INC-178 (summary; details in docs/tracking/incidental-findings.md): 160 CT-6 Escape-swallow · 161 admin_list_categories 57014 (C3d CTEs) · 162 sub-44px verb targets (touch size at the primitive) · 163 redirect latency = the i18n bundle's per-request aggregation (in-process cache by publication version) · 164 the same, root: Nano compute + 8s role timeout + nine-job concurrency · 165 listings never reaped (feed-empty world-assumption; reaper + scoped anchors) · 166 shared-identity session churn (deadlocks on /factors/verify) · 167 stale stepped-up-at hint reader (deleted) · 168 shared refresh token revoked the family (session per test) · 169 challenge-window expiry after 10 minutes (per-test verify) · 170 scratch-prefix anchors (all scratch slugs start with e2e-) · 171 C-2/C-3 inconsistent skip guard · 172 DataTable pagination slicing owned by the primitive · 173 language baseline on acquire = NULL (fresh-user state); switchLanguage waits on the device record · 174 local-only RP-1 sign-out reconciliation (open) · 175 EN baseline heal on staging (compiled EN is staging's truth of record) · 176 orphan function on the connected project after a lost working tree (migration and commit in the same turn; report divergence first) · 177 non-idempotent import round-trip (semantic option comparison; the real-export invariant) · 178 read-only reporter noise (one serializer; compare after normalization).
Slips (supervisor): prompts written from partial reads under time pressure (L4 family); "primitive-owned min-widths" instruction that opted the attributes table into the primitive's last-resort mechanism; a Lovable settings path named without verification; "commit it" phrasing that implied an operator action.
DEC-046 — The import gate: every import family (categories, attributes, translations CSV/XLIFF, any future one) enters through one server entry point (src/server/imports/gate.ts) declared in a registry — permission, step-up, file identity, column classes (identity | editable | read-only | action), per-column type/regex/length, row and byte caps; ingress checks (UTF-8, RFC-4180, exact headers), cell hygiene (NFC, control/bidi/zero-width stripped, NUL and formula-leading refused), typed JSONB to the planners, per-operator×family rate limit, audit per preview/commit/undo; the DB doors validate again and stay the sole authority; a CI guard refuses any import/export route or console writer call outside the gate; the shared hostile-file spec (e2e/import-security.spec.ts) is parameterised over every registered family.
DEC-047 — Guard cancel contract (DEC-040 amendment): guard() rejects with StepUpCancelled / StepUpUnavailable when the step-up prompt is cancelled or no factor exists, so every caller resets its pending state; proofs MF-7b/MF-7c.
DEC-048 (candidate, workflow) — Delete the inert E2E_UI_LOGIN knob from ci.yml and nightly-e2e.yml (DEC-041 made it meaningless); requires the harness DEC ritual.
INC-179 — A migration file was deleted after it had applied on the connected project; restored byte-identical; law: migration files are never deleted or edited once applied anywhere — a failed-elsewhere migration is superseded by a corrective that heals its mark.
INC-180 — Inherited echo rows collided with new direct rows in the duplicate-key check (IE-6: direct rows only).
INC-181 — Empty read-only cells reported as edits (IE-6: empty means "not provided").
INC-182 — TR-19/TR-30 anchored on page-one ordering and a bare twin testid; fixed at the anchor (J5/J7).
Scanner findings 1–5 (2026-09-09): resolved at their roots — atomic admin_update_attribute_link (AT-42), guard cancel contract (MF-7b/c), export follows the catalog (TR-33), one icon allowlist (CT-29), image generation verified working by a captured-fixture unit test.
Vehicles curation (2026-09-08/09): imported through preview → confirm; D1 overruled the handoff (Cars flat + body_style); the Vehicles root carries zero direct links; every listing category except Auto Services (deferred) has its card attributes; inheritance follows primary lineage only (INH-1).

DEC-049 (candidate, 2026-09-10) — Bump GitHub Actions `actions/cache`, `actions/upload-artifact`, `actions/download-artifact` and `gitleaks-action` to their Node-24-native major versions; pre-committed rule: next push + nightly green, else revert.

INC-183 (2026-09-10) — A migration patched a function body by text anchor (second occurrence). LAW: a migration NEVER patches a function body by anchor, flexible or not — it re-declares the function whole with `CREATE OR REPLACE`, restates its closers and reads back its definition and ACL in-file. Closed by `20260910053535_40e4ab7d`; no anchored patch remains in the lineage.

The curation thread's review note (deliverable 1) yielded fourteen decisions, ruled with this thread's recommendations: D1 transaction type is the `offer_type-real-estate` attribute, one tree by property type (filters, not layers — the body-type law); Short-term Rentals and Rooms & Shared stay categories because the product differs; D2 the two sale twins retired; D3 `condominiums` new, `apartments-condos` relabelled Apartments; D4 `new-developments` new and the single home for developer/off-plan projects — `completion_status` and `payment_plan` link only there, resale half-built units use `finishing_state`; D5 commercial stays one category with `commercial_type`; D6 `offer-type-apartments-condos` replaced by `offer_type-real-estate`; D7 root ends with zero direct links; D8 type changes by file — `bedrooms` number→single_select, `furnished` boolean→single_select, `bathrooms` number→single_select with halves (verified: `type` is an editable import column and the update RPC accepts it; no listings exist); D9 `zoning` merged into `land_use` in the console (0 links moved · 1 folded into the existing Land & Plots link · 1 definition removed); D10 `rent_period` depends_on `offer_type` (parent rent) and `payment_plan` depends_on `offer_type` (parent sale) — DEC-045 beyond make→model; D11 `amenities` is the catalog's first multi_select (supported end to end); D12 nine new definitions; D13 orphans deleted, `stories` included; D14 boundary rule, decided once: rent a property → Real Estate, book a hospitality service → Travel. Standing file rules: `is_required` true only on card rows (DEC-043); `label_am` filled for every surviving definition; `other` present on open option lists. Files validated here before preview: headers and column classes exact, depth 2, no echo-row collisions, cards ≥2 on every listing category (eight with three, the catch-all with two), dependents' parents co-present, no formula-leading cells. Console counts as shown: categories `created 2 · changed 7 · retired 2 · unchanged 2 · refused 0`; attributes `85 added · 25 changed · 15 unlinked · 6 deleted · 36 unchanged · 0 refused`, `category_path` 25 rows not applied (read-only, stale after the renames); `Import applied — 131 changes written`. Walk: tree and retirements as intended, no "Needs card attributes" on any listing category (`realtor-services` stays with the Services pass), `amenities` renders with 16 options; pending Amharic approved. Left open on the pass: category images not generated ("Missing assets" — the C5 bulk fill is a separate step); O4 slug/label mismatch kept (slugs are identities).

INC-187 — REGISTERED 2026-09-11 (walk finding; phantom-success class, F4/§7): the categories import plans `display_order` but never commits it. `cat_import_plan` carries `display_order` in every changed row's delta and in every create payload; `admin_commit_category_import` calls `admin_update_category` with NULL in `p_display_order` (the door applies `COALESCE(p_display_order, c.display_order)`, so NULL keeps the old value) and `admin_create_category` has no order parameter, so creates are appended. The export marks the column editable, so the Real Estate file's five reorders and two placed creates were counted as changes, reported as written, and quietly not applied — Condominiums 9 and New Developments 10 instead of 3 and 4; Commercial, Land and Short-term unmoved. Stale `category_path` cells are at least reported under "Ignored (read-only)"; `display_order` is dropped without a word. Fix, next execution prompt and ahead of the Electronics import: the commit applies the delta's order through `p_display_order` and follows every create with the same update carrying the payload's order; catch-all rows are skipped (the reorder door pins them at 1000000+); the categories-import round-trip E2E asserts `display_order` on changed and created rows against DB truth (J4) — the walk outranks the green test (G26). Proof: the Real Estate categories file re-imported unchanged lands the intended order.

D-GATE-8 verification — DRIFT, corrected in this landing: the INC-174 closure paragraph landed with an empty line between each of its wrapped lines (content verbatim; the line-replace paste turned single newlines into paragraph breaks, where D-GATE-7's appended blocks had landed byte-identical). Landed otherwise as specified: 152c2aa, five files, green, promoted.

NEXT: INC-187 fix → Real Estate categories file re-imported (order only) → Electronics curation import (export to the curation thread now; import after the fix) → remaining passes → Locations era spec session (DEC-033) + posting-prerequisites census → U6 Posting.

INC-187 — MECHANISM CORRECTED 2026-09-11 (supervisor slip: the registered cause "the commit never commits it" was ruled from a grep window that cut before the decisive block — the class that forged the G3 addendum; first recurrence since; logged, no new rule). Creates ignore the payload's order (appended); updates commit order one row at a time, ranking each against the siblings' current numbering and renumbering after each, so a multi-row reorder does not reproduce the file's sequence; the catch-all's order cell counts as a change the door then ignores. FIX PART 1: migration 5c1e174e re-declares `cat_import_plan`, `admin_commit_category_import` and `admin_undo_category_import` WHOLE (INC-183; closers and read-backs in-file) — no catch-all order delta; one ordering pass per primary parent after all rows, on commit and on undo. Part 2 next: CT-30 asserts the sequence on commit and its restoration on undo against DB truth; then the Real Estate categories file re-imports unchanged.

S36 addendum 4 (2026-09-11):

Attribute system review — RATIFIED 2026-09-11 (docs/governance/reviews/attribute-system-deep-dive-2026-09-11.md, imported byte-identical, sha256 b8c928d03bb1ee94cf35a688344486ecb610b9c573bea11df2675f2cfede1d6a). Read against REQ-017/018/020/022/025, DEC-043/044/045, DEC-033, the C1 taxonomy and the two finished curation passes. Finding: the six types plus inline Amharic options, one-parent dependents, cards and inheritance carry every remaining vertical to curation; posting and search need additive definition fields; bookings are a category capability plus a module, not a type.

DEC-050 — Attribute definition v2 (P1, posting prerequisite): nullable `unit`, `min`, `max`, `decimals` (0–3), `format` (`plain`|`year`) on number definitions, with relative bounds from an allowlist of tokens (`year`, `year±n`) resolved server-side; option-conditioned bounds — an option record may carry `bounds: { <sibling attribute_key>: {min?, max?} }` naming a number definition linked wherever the owner is linked, folded into the sibling's effective range (the make/model → year validity window: first production year, last year of a discontinued model, never beyond next year); option `active` (inactive: hidden from new posts, valid on existing listings, kept in filters while referenced) and `aliases` (search only, ≤5×32); text `preset` from a code allowlist (`digits:n`, `vin`, `plate-et`, `alnum:a-b`, `free:n`) plus `max_length`, never free regex, never a contact-data preset; `help_text_am` added and both hints exported/imported; `range` removed from the attr_type CHECK (a dead type; returns with Jobs v2 if ever); an option-coverage read for Amharic. All cells appended after the current definitions header so files exported before P1 still import as unchanged. Constraints are validated at import (F5) and evaluated by the DEC-051 validator only.

DEC-051 — Validation authority (U6-A): one SECURITY DEFINER `validate_listing_attributes(category_id, attrs)` is the only authority for listing attribute values (effective links, cards per DEC-043, types, options incl. `active`, dependents, DEC-050 bounds and folds, presets, multi membership, the `other` shape); `listings.attrs jsonb` holds typed values with `other` stored as `{ value: "other", text }` (C3-UX-4 solved in the shape); a definition's type change and an option's deletion are refused once any listing references them; the client mirrors the checks for UX only.

DEC-052 — Category capabilities: `capabilities text[]` on categories with a CHECK allowlist (`bookable` first), exported/imported as one cell, inherited like other category behaviour, landed at U6-A; the Bookings module — availability, reservations state machine, notifications, conversations, RLS per party, request-only under DEC-001 — is its own era (candidate U9) after U8 messaging.

DEC-053 — Option delivery: option lists are delivered per definition, lazily, version-cached (REQ-029); the 400-option cap is raised only on the Electronics note's evidence.

Standing curation rules (curation handoff §7): R1 global-before-vertical definitions — search the library first; reuse `condition`, `color`, `brand`, `warranty`, `swap_exchange`, `rate_period` where the option set matches; suffix `-<vertical>` only when the set is vertical-specific. R2 fixed unit per definition; bounded sets as single_select; open numerics as `number` with the unit in the label until DEC-050 lands, then backfilled by one import per finished vertical.

Sequencing (operator directive 2026-09-11, supersedes the morning order): Electronics import (files need none of P1) → DEC-050 Pass-2 spec → P1 landings (ESTIMATE 3–4) → backfill imports for Vehicles, Real Estate, Electronics → the eleven remaining passes with the new cells (proposed order: Fashion · Home & Garden · Services · Travel & Accommodation · Construction Material · Beauty & Personal Care · Sports & Leisure · Agriculture & Farming · Babies & Kids · Pets & Animals · Commercial Equipment; residue: Auto Services cards → Services, heavy-machinery models → Commercial Equipment / Vehicles delta) → Locations era (spec drafted in parallel; build interleaves when the curation thread is slower than the executor) → U6-A (DEC-051, DEC-052 column) → U6-B..E → U7 → U8 → Bookings era. Rationale: each vertical curated before P1 needs a backfill; each curated after fills the cells once, by the thread inside the domain.

Not built, by decision (so nobody re-derives them): a rule language for cross-attribute predicates; unit pickers; posting-form groups until a form walk asks; variants (`is_per_variant` stays, nothing built); `range`.

NEXT: Electronics curation import (counts line before Confirm) → DEC-050 Pass-2 spec session → P1 landings → backfills → the eleven remaining passes → Locations era → U6.

Number and Text groups mount per type in `components/attribute-v2-fields.tsx` (B4: the dialog was already 843 lines) and clear on a type change; `format = 'year'` disables decimals at 0; the preset builder emits only the door's tokens and serializes an incomplete builder to null; help text EN+AM at 240 with counters; every v2 field is sent on every save (the door assigns them directly on UPDATE, so an unsent field would erase a curated value — the reason L3a-mig existed). S37 · 2026-09-15 · LOCATIONS ERA SPEC SESSION (ratified)

Spec ratified and imported: /docs/governance/locations-era-spec.md (v2; sha256 32d66532…). Executes DEC-033 — the S34 U5-draft locations section carried unchanged (Ethiopia-deep tree, diaspora seed, ancestry trigger, locations console, country activation) — on the operator's rulings of 2026-09-15 (T1–T7) and a review of apex-marketplace d920852 (coverage never persisted there, browse never widens, detection via a third-party IP API — a UI and admin-tooling donor only). Era order: G0 → L1a → L1b → L1c → L2 → L3 (curator files: countries, Ethiopia-deep, diaspora) → L4a (A7 geo-guess spike) → L4b → era gate; then the posting-prerequisites census → U6 Posting. Forward-scan: every later REQ read, none contradicted; the July posting-flow one-country rule is superseded openly by DEC-064.

Laws recorded (spec §2): tree law (four levels country → region → city → sub_city; ancestor columns country_code / region_id / city_id filled by trigger); concentration law (metro-scale cities, suburbs as aliases, one union tree across communities, a place absent from the tree is a decision); country law (countries = reference root + country profile; is_active = the market is open; identity is not a market); visibility law (a node is visible iff it and every ancestor are active; path rule in the read, never cascading writes; FKs never orphaned); naming law (name_en source, other languages in entity_translations, the name_am cell a carrier via IE-4a, the column fallback made unnecessary, column drop later on operator go); aliases law; seed and curation law (files through the one gate, never migrations; the curator authors from GeoNames CC-BY offline; nothing gazetteer-like in-app; review every quarter and at every market opening); country profile law (DEC-055 first landing); coverage law (DEC-064); guess law (DEC-063); stream law (DEC-065); persistence law (ethio_area preference cookie, profile column at U7, the guess never written); weight law (one ETag'd JSON per country; zero location queries on open).

DEC-033 — EXECUTING 2026-09-15: era opened at S37 with the landing map above.

DEC-055 — first landing named 2026-09-15: unit_system, currency_code and display_order land on the country profile at L1a; values ride the L3 countries file; canonical storage and the viewer toggle stay at U6-C/U7.

DEC-062 (2026-09-15): E2E global-setup reaper gains scratch-location reap — slug LIKE 'e2e-%', older than the DEC-036 window, children before parents, delete-only, real rows and fence languages untouched, the batch's location_import_revisions with it. Named harness delta under G22; pre-committed rule: reap is delete-only and nothing outside the e2e- prefix is touched.

DEC-063 (2026-09-15): Geo-guess source law — the visitor's starting area is a first guess from the edge's request geo facts (Cloudflare: country, region code, city): no third-party IP API, no edge function (INC-096); mapped to the curated tree country by code → region by iso_3166_2 → city/sub-city by slug or alias → else the deepest level matched; nothing matched = no guess, never a default; recomputed per request, never persisted, never a security input. Pre-committed judge for the L4a spike: cf object reachable → country + region + city; else CF-IPCountry present → country only; else none with all-null facts and no other fallback.

DEC-064 (2026-09-15): Coverage plans — a listing's coverage is a set of tree nodes at any level (listing_locations) plus an optional worldwide record; limits are admin-set per advertisement type (promotion tier per REQ-024) in coverage_plans (max_cities, max_regions, max_countries, allow_everywhere), seeded free = 1/1/1/off and changed in the console without code; posting prefills preferred posting location → last listing → the guess, always editable; U6-A enforces the plan server-side and retires A2's one-country trigger by named DEC. Supersedes the July posting-flow §Step 1 one-country rule as a hard law. All free in v1; the tiers are the pay-ready seam.

DEC-065 (2026-09-15): Stream law — the feed is one continuous nearest-first stream: the viewer's area, then the rest of the region, then the rest of the country, then worldwide, each section labelled, every card showing its place, no question and no click; match = a coverage node contains the scope or the scope contains it; nearest-first by center distance always; the manual override outranks the ladder. U7 builds the query; the Locations era guarantees coordinates on every city and sub-city, geo_distance_km, and the ancestor columns.

REQ-005 NOTE (2026-09-15): four levels; seed by file; the stream replaces the tier switch (honest labels kept); the saved area wins over the guess; the guess is never persisted; markets = open countries.

REQ-018 NOTE (2026-09-15): currency_code on the country profile is the poster's default currency; conversion display and rates remain DEC-056 at U7.

REQ-023 NOTE (2026-09-15): country_root_order is the per-country cold-start rail order (at home Vehicles · Real Estate · Electronics lead; abroad Food · Fashion · Beauty · Real Estate · Travel lead); a later learner treats it as a pin.

REQ-024 NOTE (2026-09-15): coverage limits per promotion tier (DEC-064) are the coverage-pricing seam; promotion records unchanged.

REQ-034 NOTE (2026-09-15): the tos-privacy-source ledger gains A14 (third-party data attribution: GeoNames CC BY 4.0) and B12 (preference cookies: language star and chosen area; no personal data).

S39 · 2026-09-16 · U6 POSTING SPEC SESSION (ratified) + POSTING-PREREQUISITES CENSUS + MEDIA STORAGE

Ratified and imported: /docs/governance/u6-posting-spec.md (v2; sha256 094db2ef…), /docs/governance/posting-prerequisites-census.md (sha256 9ffe0e7a…), /docs/governance/media-storage-memo.md (sha256 0bca444b…). The census closes the question the 2026-09-11 queue correction asked: nothing precedes U6-A — the home-country and contact-channel capture live inside the first posting flow by law (REQ-006; identity-schema deferral); tags are U7 (REQ-041); brands are attribute options; the curator's two location files proceed in parallel and close the Locations era gate on import. Era order: G1 → A1 → A2 → B1 → B2 → C1 → C2 → D1 → D2 → E1 → E2 → gate. Forward-scan: REQ-023–041 read, none contradicted; two July locks amended by operator ruling (below).

Rulings 2026-09-16 (D1–D10): AI assistance for title/description from day one under the grounded-only law with a deterministic fake for tests (July manual-v1 lock amended); the map pin is a category capability (`map_pin`), lazy-loaded, and a seller's shop pin arrives with storefronts (REQ-008); a price period per category (once·hour·day·week·month·year) with ranges as attributes; screening on Gemini via the provisioned key with dials tuned toward automation, restricted categories always held, strikes, auto-removal, retroactive sweeps and appeals; residency = three facts, one rule (observed country at the first contribution, immutable; declared home country as preference; each post's partition key = the observed country at that post; mismatch a review signal; the rule in `residency_country_for`); contact preference as a profile shape (messages always; phone/Telegram/WhatsApp optional, show/hide); photos stripped of hidden metadata on the server by keeping only image data, variants made on the phone, one optional YouTube link per listing never hosted; the expiry job is the background expiry/reminder job (pg_cron if exposed, else the nightly's scheduler); tags at U7; a duplicate-post soft warning.

DEC-067 (2026-09-16): Price period law — `listings.price_period`, `categories.default_price_period` + `price_period_locked`; ranges are attributes; free/contact carry no amount.

DEC-068 (2026-09-16): Residency three-facts law — `user_directory.observed_country_code` (server-set at the first contribution, immutable), `home_country_code` (declared, editable, defaults only), every listing's partition key set by `residency_country_for(user, request)` to the observed country at that post; never from the client; observed ≠ declared is a review signal.

DEC-069 (2026-09-16): Photo strip law — the server strip route keeps only image data (JPEG APPn/COM/XMP, WebP EXIF/XMP/ICC, PNG text chunks dropped), verifies the real format by magic bytes, is the only writer of `exif_stripped = true`, and is proven by captured fixtures with GPS/XMP/ICC before any upload ships (REQ-036 acceptance); the phone makes the three variants.

DEC-070 (2026-09-16): Three-tier screening — draft → screening → live | reduced | rejected (appeal) | held; one gateway route (structure → sanitise → files → content-policy model); thresholds are dials tuned toward automation; restricted categories held; strikes on `standing` shorten allowances and suspend at a dial; a re-screen sweep applies rule/model updates retroactively; every verdict audited with model, version and rationale; a CI guard proves no write path bypasses the gateway.

DEC-071 (2026-09-16): Rate-limit primitive — `rate_limits` + `consume_rate_limit(action, key, limit, window)`; applied to post/draft, edit, upload, report, message, resend; refusals name the window; never client-only.

DEC-072 (2026-09-16): AI assist from day one — `/api/listings/assist` drafts title/description ONLY from the seller's facts (category, attributes, declared photo facts); never quality claims; seller accepts, edits or writes; deterministic fake mode in E2E.

DEC-073 (2026-09-16): Video link law — one optional YouTube URL per listing, validated to the YouTube shape, stored as a URL, never hosted or embedded in the feed; thumbnail on the card, player on tap through the no-cookie domain; screened like text.

DEC-074 (2026-09-16): Automated enforcement — human involvement is the exception; confident violations auto-reject or auto-remove; strikes, suspension at a dial, retroactive sweeps, user reports feeding the same queue, appeals on every automatic action.

DEC-075 (2026-09-16): Media storage — one storage adapter routes every media write and URL by partition (keys `<partition>/<user_id>/<listing_id>/<photo_id>/<variant>`; the database stores partition + key, never a provider URL; the strip route is the only writer); rest-of-world on Cloudflare R2 through its S3 API (zero egress); Supabase Storage stays for admin assets; the Ethiopia partition (in-country object storage beside an in-country database — Ethio Telecom Cloud if its S3 API checks out, else MinIO on Wingu/Raxio colocation, served through Cloudflare's Addis edge) is DESIGNED now and stood up on the licensing trigger. OPERATOR RULING: one physical partition at launch, images included, for as long as the platform operates without an Ethiopian licence (ESTIMATE 1–3 years); the split must then be a configuration and a copy by predicate, never a redesign. Counsel questions added to Q-014: photos as personal data; edge caching as transfer; ECA registration; the proclamation's reach over a controller without an Ethiopian establishment.

REQ-033 NOTE (2026-09-16): the launch shape is amended — ONE physical partition at launch; the Ethiopia partition stands up on the licensing trigger (a licensing-trigger checklist replaces the launch-gate partition item); the only partition item left on the launch gate is a staging rehearsal of the copy-by-predicate split proving it is configuration plus a copy.

REQ-018 NOTE (2026-09-16): price period per category (DEC-067). REQ-019 NOTE: one optional YouTube link (DEC-073); variants on the phone. REQ-020 NOTE: AI assist from day one, grounded-only (DEC-072); the home country and contact preference captured inside the first posting. REQ-021 NOTE: the gateway is DEC-070's route; REQ-022 NOTE: the screening states join the state machine; REQ-036 NOTE: DEC-069 is the implementation and its fixtures the acceptance; REQ-038 NOTE: `search_tsv` from day one; DEC-071 is the rate-limit primitive.

DEC-076 (2026-09-23): The sign-in door and the expired prior session — a deliberate sign-in SUPERSEDES an expired session: the expired session is evicted (local sign-out + its INC-223 clocks) BEFORE the credentials are sent, so no stale refresh remains to race; a live session is never touched, because a failed sign-in must not sign anybody out; and the door reports success only once the new session is committed to storage and readable, a grant that never commits being an honest failure with one `[ssr-error]` line rather than a silent redirect to a signed-out marketplace (INC-266).

Fixture-proven (the captured results with the first test flipped to flaky; a replayed 11-flip for the cap). Rule (pre-committed): kept if the next three runs that carry a flake show one body per FLAKY line and the report stays under 200 KB; else git revert. Reason: 217 retried-then-passed lines in seven days and nine tests over DEC-030's threshold with no body to root-cause from (G21).

DEC-080 (2026-09-27, operator "flag, go") — THE HOME OF A CATEGORY IS AN EXPLICIT FLAG. `category_tree_pointers.is_primary` marks exactly one pointer per child as its home (partial unique index `category_tree_pointers_one_home`); a first pointer is the home (BEFORE INSERT trigger `cat_pointer_default_home`); deleting a home promotes the child's next pointer (AFTER DELETE trigger `cat_pointer_promote_home`; the door audits `was_home`); `cat_primary_pointer`/`cat_primary_parent` read the flag first (legacy order as fallback for a retired home); `admin_move_category_pointer` MERGES onto a parent already holding the child (audit `merged`); NEW `admin_set_primary_pointer(uuid)` (restructure + step-up); the import and undo ordering passes rank by (child, parent) — own children first, guests after — and can never move a home; a file's `parent_slug` IS the home and `secondary_parents` the guests; `get_category_tree_version` covers every pointer's parent, order and flag; the public tree route serves `is_primary`, and the client sorts roots by their parent-NULL pointer and every level by pointer order (the row column `categories.display_order` is dead to readers). Rationale: the ordering pass of the 2026-09-27 categories import ranked a guest pointer by its home row's rank (lookup by child id only) and "the home is the lowest-numbered pointer anywhere" then flipped four homes (INC-303); the undo could not reverse it (INC-304); the marketplace had never shown a reorder (INC-305). Repair by slug in the same migration: bicycles → vehicles, personal-care-services → services, industrial-equipment → commercial-equipment, nursery-furniture → babies-kids. Rejected alternative: a numbers-only patch (leaves the hazard for every future drag or import).
- C1 — LANDED CLEAN (98d2cd2c; migration 20260928002133_f98b5cf0, mark 20260928010000 applied prod + staging, read back; run 36362240778 attempt 2, promoted). Proofs P1–P6 + ACL read-back in-file; prod read-back: the four homes by flag, P5 = 0 children without exactly one home, version moved. Tests CT-33, C-5, PW-62, category-tree unit tests DEC-080 (i)–(iv). Executor corrections accepted: the version formula's root pointer needs `coalesce(parent_id, 'root')` (the brief's formula would have blanked the stamp); nursery-furniture's flip direction was reversed in the brief's WHY (the repair list was right).
- C1-b — LANDED CLEAN (a441db3f; migration 20260928013646_8ea67f86, mark 20260928020000 applied prod + staging, read back; run 36366774220 attempt 2, promoted). INC-306: attr_export_payload's path walk, attr_link_path and attr_link_origin re-declared whole through `cat_primary_parent`; proofs P7 (tie and lower-number guest: the three readers agree) and P8 (six slugs); AT-64.

Rail and wizard now show the study's order (verified by the operator on the published URL).

CONVENTIONS OF THIS BLOCK. Dates of operator statements are America/New_York (UTC-04:00); commit, CI-run and migration stamps are UTC, so an evening decision of 2026-09-27 lands in commits dated 2026-09-28. "Turn" means one executor turn (one prompt, one landing). Every landing below was verified by the supervisor in a fresh clone of `dev`; "promoted" means the green run fast-forwarded `main`. Every migration was applied to ethio-prod by the executor during its turn and to ethio-staging by the operator in the SQL editor ("apply <fragment> → expect mark <value>"), after which the failed jobs were re-run.

THE PRICE PAGE — DEC-081, built in five turns, all CLEAN by 2026-09-28:
- D62-1, the database half (`6ebe04ea`, run 36375370211, promoted; migration `20260928034942_247a3ed0` → mark `20260928040000`). `listings.price_negotiable boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false`; `listings_price_mode_check` allows only fixed, free, contact, commission (0 listings carried the old mode); `currencies` gains `symbol` and `display_order`, seeded for 28 codes (USD 1, ETB 2, EUR 3, GBP 4, CAD 5, AED 6, SAR 7, KES 8, AUD 9, SEK 10, NOK 11, CHF 12, ZAR 13, DJF 14, SOS 15, ERN 16, UGX 17, TZS 18, SDG 19, INR 20, CNY 21, TRY 22, QAR 23, KWD 24, OMR 25, BHD 26, ILS 27, JPY 28; any other code 900); `validate_listing_attributes` gains `p_defer_keys` so the pricing basis is judged at step 5, not on the specifications step; seven functions re-declared whole with `p_price_negotiable` as a last-place defaulted argument and the old overloads dropped; proofs P1–P7; the draft route sends `priceNegotiable === true`; test PR-11. The executor corrected its own report twice: the four new "signed-in users can run this function" linter warnings were not matched to function names, and the proofs' individual notices were not seen (the migration completed, and the proofs raise on failure). The proofs borrowed an existing active seller and set a country on it temporarily, rolled back ("Production has no seller with a recorded country").
- D62-2, the client half (`0bbf9eb7`, run 36379492978, promoted; 23 files; no migration). The leaf's pricing-basis attribute is the first question on the price step and is no longer drawn on the specifications step (the same specifications component, `only=[basisKey]` on step 5 and `exclude` on step 3); "Negotiable" stops being a price type and becomes the switch `post-price-negotiable` ("Price is negotiable" / "ዋጋው የሚደራደር ነው", key `post.price.negotiableToggle`), shown for fixed and commission prices and cleared by free or contact; a "Negotiable" badge on the listing card, the review page, both previews and the impersonation table; currency rows read "CODE · symbol — Name", the seller's home currency first, then `display_order`; the preselect chain is saved → last listing → the seller's home country → IP guess → ETB; `post.price.mode.negotiable` retired; tests PW-63, PW-64, PW-65. The executor found and fixed a bug on the way: the basis control on the price step could wipe the seller's answer, so that copy of the form now only records the basis. Catalogue rule adopted with it: a basis attribute never carries `facts` or `allowed` of its own (the supervisor's audit sweeps it).
- D62-1b, the hotfix for INC-309 (`394162bb`; migration `20260928054510_702a2975` → mark `20260928060000`). `negotiable` is an alias at every step; unmapped door errors get words; tests PR-12, PW-66; proofs P8–P10. The executor's census found the live `validate_listing_draft` body (md5 `390fb259…`) identical to the D62-1 file, so no unplanned change had touched it; after the fix it is `03df87b1…`. First disposition DRIFT, test-only: run 36383469726 was red on PW-66 alone (INC-311) and `main` stayed at `0bbf9eb7`.
- D62-1c (`9690a9d3`, run 36388786851, all 25 jobs green, promoted; no migration). PW-66 falls back instead of continuing (INC-311); the refusal summary omits control-less fields (INC-313); a read-only attribution of the price-pair exceptions (INC-312). This green board also closed D62-1b and DEC-082 on the supervisor's side.
- D62-1d (`1f7e0869`, run 36391560866 attempt 2, 25 of 25 jobs, promoted; migration `20260928072330_07bf3052` → mark `20260928090000`). `listings_price_pair_check` lets a commission draft carry no percentage until step 5 (INC-312); tests PW-67, PR-13; proofs P11, P12a, P12b, P13. No client change, so no Publish.

DEC-081 (2026-09-27, operator "approve D62 as written") — THE PRICE PAGE. (A) Negotiable is a toggle beside the amount, stored as `listings.price_negotiable`, with a "Negotiable" badge beside the price on the card, the detail and the admin table and a feed filter; `negotiable` stops being a price mode. (B) The leaf's pricing-basis attribute ("per hour / per job / fixed / quote") is asked on the price step, above the amount, not on Specifications; the doors judge it at step 5; D44 locks and prefills are unchanged; Review shows it under Price. (C) Currency: the default chain is saved → last listing → the seller's home country's currency → IP country → ETB; the list opens with that currency first, USD second, then a curated commonness order; every row shows code, symbol and name; `currencies` gains `symbol` and `display_order`, so opening Kenya, Somalia, Djibouti or Eritrea costs one row each in `countries.currency_code`. Rationale: the operator's ruling that the price page is where a seller naturally says the price is negotiable, that the currency of the country the seller posts from must come first with the most common currency (usually USD) next, and that symbols help recognition. This is ruling D62 of the previous block; DEC-081 had been reserved for it. Status (2026-10-04): built and landed in the five turns above (`6ebe04ea`, `0bbf9eb7`, `394162bb`, `9690a9d3`, `1f7e0869`; marks `20260928040000`, `20260928060000`, `20260928090000` on prod and staging). Deferred piece: the feed's "negotiable only" filter chip waits for the feed-filters spec (no filter surface exists yet). Later amendments recorded elsewhere: on 2026-09-30 part (B) was amended for goods (a `unit_of_sale-` basis is asked on the specifications step; service bases stay on the price step) — an amendment itself reversed by DEC-109 (2026-10-02): the unit of sale is asked on the price page again (N2 retired, DEC-121) — and bundle 4 (2026-10-04) moved the price page ahead of the title page (decided 2026-10-02 as DEC-110 "TITLE AND DESCRIPTION COME AFTER THE PRICE", block S50; the door's step numbers follow as DEC-120, block S51; screens A + B landed 2026-10-04, `roadmap.md` "Screens A + B — done (steps 1–11; PW-135, PW-136–139 …)").

CATEGORY IMAGES — INC-308 AND DEC-082. The executor's read-only investigation could not capture the failing call (it cannot sign in as an admin: the Supabase project is external to it) but established that the route's own refusals are JSON and that the pipeline burns 130–180 ms of CPU per image on the Worker. The supervisor froze the decision rule before the operator's capture: "if the body is a Cloudflare error page (or anything non-JSON), DEC-082 = option (a)". The capture showed the hosting platform's HTML error page, and the rule decided. The build turn landed as `0bf75959` (Tier B, no migration, no policy change; its own run 36382822067 was cancelled by the next push, and the first green board carrying it is `9690a9d3`): the route keeps the gate, the body checks, the category read, the prompt and the provider call and returns `{ stage: "generated", prompt, genMs, image: <base64> }` with `Cache-Control: no-store`; new `src/features/admin-categories/category-image-variants.ts` cuts the card (512), thumb (128) and OG (1200×630) variants on canvas with the old pipeline's constants; the service uploads `card-<ts>.png`, `thumb-<ts>.png` and `og-<ts>.png` under `<categoryId>/` to the bucket `category-assets` under the admin's session and `admin_set_category_images` saves the links; failures carry a stage (model-call, process, upload, persist, client-timeout); `pipeline.ts`, `raster.ts`, `raster.test.ts` and `font.ts` are deleted and declared in `docs/tracking/intentional-deletions.txt`; tests CI-1, CI-2, CI-2b, CI-4b, CI-4 rewritten to the new contract. pngjs, jpeg-js and @types/pngjs stay in `package.json` because `scripts/fixtures/photos/make.ts` imports them. Executor limitations recorded: its sandbox holds a real Gemini key and the first local test run spent a few image credits; the real provider sends JPEG, not PNG (CI-2's PNG check holds only in fake mode). Operator acceptance walk 2026-09-28: "11 generated · 0 failed".

DEC-082 (2026-09-27, decided by a rule frozen before the evidence) — CATEGORY IMAGE VARIANTS ARE CUT IN THE ADMIN'S BROWSER; THE WORKER ONLY CALLS THE PROVIDER. The generate route calls the image provider and returns the generated image; the admin console cuts the card, thumb and OG variants in the browser and uploads them under the admin's own session; the existing door `admin_set_category_images` saves the links. No migration and no storage-policy change were needed: holders of `categories:assets` could already write to `category-assets` (policies in `20260903114705`, public read in `20260903105611`). Rationale: bulk "Generate image" died in the hosting layer with an HTML 500 (INC-308); whether the Worker ran out of CPU decoding the image or failed loading the image libraries, the cure is the same — no image processing and no image library on the Worker. Rejected: the two other options examined — (b) a Supabase Edge Function doing the processing, (c) a provider-side resize. Status (2026-10-04): built and landed (`0bf75959`; green board at `9690a9d3`, run 36388786851); accepted by the operator's walk of 2026-09-28. Leftovers closed in W1: INC-310 and the stale sentence in `docs/features/categories.md`.

Part B: 21 icon names join the category icon allowlist (136 names) and the categories import refuses an unlisted icon as `unknownIcon` (INC-316). Part C: the image prompt's parent is the home pointer (INC-310). Docs: the icon rule and the DEC-082 wording in `docs/features/categories.md`. First-paint JavaScript 272.4 of 320 KiB (about 2.0 KiB more than after DEC-082), CSS 15.5 of 40 KiB. The run's first two attempts were red from the staging outage; the green attempt carried 89 post-test warnings (teardown deferrals from the outage window).

STAGING OUTAGE (INC-318, 2026-09-28). ethio-staging answered 522 and read Unhealthy; the operator's fast database reboot did not clear it, the full project restart did, and the re-run went green. Platform origin; cause not found in the Postgres log excerpt. The same excerpt produced INC-319 (a `profiles.id` error with no source in the project).

INC-317, THE PW-61 FLAKE — ROOT CAUSE IN THE APP, FIXED IN THREE TAKES. The investigation turn (`c34b727b`, changelog line only) found a real-seller bug: a late currency prefill queued a step no longer on screen. Take 1 (queue reads the step on screen, plus dropping a currency answer that arrives after the price step closed) failed its own repeat run — PW-72 as written 24 of 24 (a test fault) and PW-61 "Undo within ten seconds" 19 of 24 (real: the dropped write was what mirrored the door's own currency fill) — and the executor's "undo" restored the already-committed version (`7a020e55`), so `dev` carried the failed attempt. Take 2 (`18f1b592`, run 36414969833 red) restored `step-pricing.tsx` and kept only `stepRef`; PW-72's second assertion was wrong (a step-4 autosave on photos is legitimate once details was finished). Take 3 (`de56b6c8`, run 36418421258, 25 of 25, promoted) rewrote PW-72 as a deterministic test on the D59 reset path and applied a baseline judge frozen in advance: "the fix is not a regression if every (test, project) count on the current tree is >= baseline − 1; PW-72 must be 24/24". It passed; the full wizard suite read 137 passed, 3 skipped, 0 failed at 4 workers. The baseline also produced INC-322.
