SERVER-ERROR CENSUS AND ACCESSIBILITY PASS — DEC-083, DEC-084, AND THE CORRECTION TURN R1. On 2026-09-28 the operator asked whether every fix ships with tests "so we will not have silent failurews next time". The supervisor named two real gaps — swallowed server exceptions on green runs (INC-312 was visible only because its run happened to be red) and no automated accessibility pass — plus the never-read list of 165 security-scan warnings, and proposed DEC-083, DEC-084 and a read-only census. The harness turn landed on `694689f6` (Tier B, named DECs under guardrail G22; files `docs/tracking/ssr-error-allowlist.txt`, `e2e/a11y.spec.ts`, `docs/tracking/security-scan-2026-09-28.md`, `@axe-core/playwright` pinned exact). Its run 36435962253 was red in the Build job (INC-326) and exposed a blind spot: CI uploaded browser-test logs only when a job failed, so on a green run both new sections read "No … lines (or no log was uploaded)" — the check was blind and read as clean (the executor's report had named the smoke-log half of this as a limitation). R1 ("the red and the blind census"; `4e2a9aad`, run 36494534782, green at 2026-09-28 23:09 UTC, promoted) fixed both: the `.prettierignore` entry; the smoke, shard and email log-capture steps run `if: always()`; the a11y tests are tagged `@a11y` and the shards run `--grep-invert "@global-state|@a11y"`, so the pass runs once, in the smoke tier; each section opens with "Logs read: <sources> · unavailable: <sources or none>" and never states zero for a log it could not read. The only red job in that run was the fast lane, which is signal-only; its report showed no failure behind the red (watch: a second unexplained fast-lane red gets an incident). The first real census read "Logs read: all 9 · unavailable: none" and listed six messages off the allowlist: "listing not found" ×102 (INC-323), the card-rank duplicate key (INC-327) and four deliberate refusals later attributed to their tests in W2. The first accessibility result was `serious=1 critical=0` (INC-325). Three clarifications were sent mid-turn: the verbatim rule texts (S65), an accessibility roster of existing pages only (there is no public listing page, no storefront, and sign-in is at `/auth`), and seeding the allowlist by attributing each message to its spec from evidence already in the repo's history, because the operator never attaches logs.

DEC-083 (2026-09-28, operator "please proceed with your proposal") — SERVER-ERROR CENSUS ON EVERY RUN. Text as recorded in `docs/features/e2e-harness.md`: "DEC-083 — Server-error census (2026-09-28). Every run, green or red, writes a "Server errors — census" section: [ssr-error] lines from every source, counted by normalised message. Messages on docs/tracking/ssr-error-allowlist.txt are counted but quiet; an allowlist entry must name the test that provokes it, and a message no test can be shown to provoke stays off the list. Gating rule, pre-committed: the census is non-gating for 14 days from 2026-09-28. During that window any SQL-class line on a green run (SQLSTATE classes 22, 23 or 42 — messages containing "violates", "does not exist" or "null value") receives an INC the same day. From 2026-10-12, once five consecutive runs have carried zero off-allowlist SQL-class lines, the merged verdict fails on any new off-allowlist SQL-class line; a message added to the allowlist after that date needs a DEC." Rationale: the failure reporter quoted `[ssr-error]` lines only for a failed source (capped at 20), so a swallowed exception on a green run was invisible. The number DEC-083 had first been reserved, earlier the same day, for the D66 publish attestation "when its prompt ships"; that reservation lapsed when the number was used here, and D66 never received a DEC number in this period. Status (2026-10-04): built and live, non-gating (`694689f6`, corrected by R1 `4e2a9aad`); gating becomes possible from 2026-10-12, once five consecutive runs have carried zero off-allowlist SQL-class lines.

DEC-084 (2026-09-28, operator "please proceed with your proposal") — ACCESSIBILITY PASS (AXE-CORE). Text as recorded: "DEC-084 — Accessibility pass (2026-09-28). The smoke tier runs axe-core on the marketplace home, /auth, and wizard steps 1, 3 and 5, at mobile-360 and desktop-1280, and the report carries serious and critical counts per page and project. The public listing page and the seller storefront join the roster in the turn that builds each of them; a build prompt for either page that does not add it is incomplete. Gating rule, pre-committed: non-gating until five consecutive runs have carried zero serious and zero critical violations; from the sixth such run, the merged verdict fails on any new serious or critical violation. A violation may be exempted only by a DEC naming the rule id and the page." (A first text naming "one listing page, one storefront, sign-in" was superseded the same day, because those pages do not exist.) Status (2026-10-04): built (`e2e/a11y.spec.ts`, tests A11Y-1 and A11Y-2, `@axe-core/playwright` pinned at 4.13.0) and GATING since 2026-09-29: the rule was met by seven consecutive clean runs (`611e1094` → `4de0e33f`) and W5 (`d25b5d79`) made the spec fail on any serious or critical violation. Open: the reporter's section heading still reads "Accessibility (DEC-084, non-gating)" at dev `48d3c53b` (`scripts/e2e-failure-report.ts:514`; the report of run 37264070069, 2026-10-05, prints it); the public listing page and the storefront must join the roster when they are built.

SECURITY-SCAN CENSUS (2026-09-28, read-only, `docs/tracking/security-scan-2026-09-28.md`). Every later executor report that mentions "165 older warnings" refers to this ruled set.

STANDING RULE G29, used as a numbered rule in every executor prompt from W4 on: "for every part, find and fix every instance of the same defect class across the app, not only the one named; list what you checked". It carries the operator's standing preference of 2026-09-29 that no issue is fixed as one instance only. It is not in the installed supervisor instructions v1.12, which end at G28. It was numbered in turn 925 (2026-09-29, after the operator's 02:15 directive of turn 924): "I'll also add it as a standing rule, G29, in the next update of the supervisor instructions, so every future thread follows it", with the two consequences "Every Lovable prompt that fixes a finding starts with a check of the whole app for the same problem, and fixes every instance it finds" and "Every curator brief reviews the whole catalogue against the problem types, as C27 now does" (raw turns now available).

Part B: PW-74, the round-trip law — fill every control type, go forward, come Back: every control shows its answer, no Undo banner, the stored attributes equal the filled set; the prompt ordered evidence first, and the test failed on the unfixed code before the fix. KNOWN PRODUCTS ASK ONLY WHAT VARIES — DEC-085, DEC-086, W4 AND W5. After the W3 walk the operator found that a fully known product (iPhone 13) still asked battery capacity, screen size, display and RAM, and restated the rule for every category: minimise what the seller fills for a known product, and where only one option exists keep it and do not allow changing it.

DEC-085 (2026-09-29, supervisor decision on the operator's known-product directive) — ONE RULE FOR EVERY FIELD TYPE: SETTLED MEANS FILLED AND HIDDEN. A detail the chosen product fixes to one value is filled and hidden, whether it is a pick-list, a number, a year or a yes/no; the review page and the preview still show it and the door still judges it. A fact that is only the most common variant (a storage default, for example) stays editable (operator ruling of 2026-09-20). Yes/no facts need a small addition to the catalogue format so the curator can lock them. Rationale: the operator's earlier rulings (D44 of 2026-09-22, and 2026-09-27) had reached only one kind of field, a pick-list narrowed to exactly one answer. Measured by the supervisor on its copy of the catalogue: 240 numbers fixed to one value were filled but shown editable (iPhone 13 battery, 3,227 mAh), 246 years fixed to one value were not even filled (release year 2021), and 307 yes/no facts could not be locked (306 of them "dual SIM"). Status (2026-10-04): built for numbers and years in W4 (`4de0e33f`; `pinnedNumber`; test PW-76); the yes/no lock, and the same fill-and-hide for a link scope that leaves one value, were assigned to a turn named W4b that was never issued — `docs/features/posting.md` still reads "Yes/no pins are deferred to W4b" at dev `48d3c53b` (line 1079); W4b was never issued and is named in no later source (blocks S46–S53).

DEC-086 (2026-09-29, operator "big YES") — THE EXACT-MODEL QUESTION IS REQUIRED. An exact-model question is required whenever the chosen series or make offers more than one model. The seller can still pick "Other" when the model is not listed; then no specs fill in and the seller answers them. The database enforces it when a draft is saved, not only the form. The rule is defined by behaviour, not by name: a dependent pick-list (a definition with `depends_on`) whose options carry `facts`, `allowed` or `bounds` for other details is required once its parent is answered and the parent's chosen value offers more than one child option; the two card questions stay mandatory as before. Rationale: a known product's fixed specs sit on the model question, which was optional, so a seller who skipped it was asked everything; a seller always knows the exact model, and that one answer removes several questions. Status (2026-10-04): built and landed. Door: `validate_listing_draft` re-declared whole from its base `702a2975` in `20260929093107_1dc182d8` → mark `20260929180000` on prod and staging (`58bfd6d7`, run 36549793568; 51 lines added, none removed; proofs P19–P21); at step 3 and later an empty such list is refused `{attr_key, reason: 'required'}`; as landed the list must also be visible, and the count is of active, allowed child options ("Other" counts). Form mirror (`requiredByModel`: required mark and soft border from the moment the rule applies) and route test PR-16 in `4de0e33f`; big lists covered by INC-336 in W5. Accepted consequence: the series level is also required where a brand has several series with fixed specs (Apple → iPhone 13).

W4 — LANDED CLEAN in two commits (Tier A door, one migration). Parts A (door) and F: `58bfd6d7`, run 36549793568, promoted. Parts A (form mirror), B, C, D1, D2: `4de0e33f`, run 36561472744, promoted. Part B: DEC-085 for numbers and years (PW-76, scratch catalogue only). Part C: INC-331 could not be reproduced (PW-74 extended); no code change. Part D1: the step-1 required mark sits on the category list heading, not on the optional search box, and clears with the soft border when a leaf is chosen, also after Back (PW-75 updated). Part D2: the scroll to the first refused field is smooth (respecting reduced motion) and brings the field's label fully into view below the sticky header at 360 px before focusing the control (PW-77). Part F: comment labels INC-331 → INC-330. One difference between form and door was accepted: the form decides whether a model list's options fix anything by looking only at the options the leaf allows, the door at the whole list; no catalogue row is affected. `post-wizard-specs` reached 25 tests per project, the limit. PW-25 (case 2) and PW-75 were updated because they asserted the old rules. The executor's local DEC-023 run: 189 passed, 3 skipped, 0 failed. The executor found INC-332 while writing PW-77. Operator walk 2026-09-29: accepted with one miss (INC-336).

W5 — LANDED CLEAN (`d25b5d79`, run 36585975894, promoted; Tier B, no migration; push to report 14 minutes). Part A: INC-336 (PW-78, a 205-option list). Part B: INC-332 (PW-79). Part C: INC-323 — `stopPageBeforePurge` in `e2e/helpers/posting.ts`; the executor's local runs logged 9 and then 6 lines and missed the target, CI met it (4 lines, 2 of them PR-15's). CI-T1 — LANDED CLEAN (`7c300b6e`, run 36547403556, all 25 jobs, promoted; Tier B; no migration, no app change intended). Part A: both forms of the E2E report gain "## Timing (DEC-087, non-gating)" — wall time per source, summed time per spec file, the 15 slowest tests, a "Results read / unavailable" line; self-test on a real results file. Part B: the two largest spec files are split by area into nine, every test body byte-identical: `post-wizard-category` 20, `post-wizard-specs` 23, `post-wizard-pricing` 13, `post-wizard-resets` 8, `post-wizard-place` 12, `admin-attributes-library` 20, `admin-attributes-import` 16, `admin-attributes-links` 11, `admin-attributes-editor` 17 tests; 922 listed tests before and after (shard set 901); tags unchanged (`@global-state` 16, `@a11y` 4, `@private-identity` 34); the attribute console's shared helpers moved to `e2e/helpers/admin-attributes.ts`. Before the split, measured locally at 2 workers per shard, the six shards took 12.8, 19.6, 4.7, 14.0, 20.3 and 5.3 minutes; `post-wizard.spec.ts` alone was about 12.5 minutes of shards 2 and 5. The platform committed the executor's unfinished split and CI ran on it: red on `425039fa` (run 36533275066) for two reasons — the "Unexplained deletions" guard (INC-076: the two deleted spec files were not declared; now in `docs/tracking/intentional-deletions.txt`) and PW-10 failing on both projects, which turned out to be a real bug and not the split (INC-330; fix in `src/features/posting/pricing-data.ts`, in the same landing). The executor reported that live references to the old file names were updated class-wide (`docs/tracking/ssr-error-allowlist.txt`, `docs/features/attributes.md`, `docs/features/posting.md`), but the repo does not carry it: at dev `48d3c53b` `ssr-error-allowlist.txt` lines 17, 18 and 21 still cite `admin-attributes.spec.ts`, `docs/features/attributes.md:136` still reads "`e2e/admin-attributes.spec.ts` — AT-1 gating · AT-2 definitions · AT-3 …" and `docs/features/posting.md:162` still reads "## Tests (`e2e/post-wizard.spec.ts`)". The commit that made those edits, `3b03ef30` "Added fake mode, updated refs" (2026-09-29 07:58:13Z; 4 files: the three above and `package.json`), sits with `6b8e303a` "Work in progress" only on the platform branch `origin/lovable-sync-1790668701` (forked at `425039fa`) and is not an ancestor of dev — the first stranded executor turn, a day before INC-359, never recorded as such. Ratified governance specs and history lines keep the old names on purpose (INC-007). `.prettierignore` gained `scripts/fixtures/e2e-timing/` outside the brief's scope — accepted under the INC-326 rule and logged as a deviation. The turn was closed on CI evidence (the timing section of the green run is the after-table), without the hour-long local six-shard repeat. The executor also reported that the local-run script `e2e:local` now forces fake image mode; the repo's dev does not show that — the `package.json` change is in the same stranded commit `3b03ef30` on `origin/lovable-sync-1790668701` (see above) and `GEMINI_FAKE=1` enters dev's `e2e:local` only on 2026-10-02 (commit `b77390ea`, INC-386).

DEC-087 (2026-09-29, operator "tests are taking longer than 20 mins and as said before we need to improve that") — CI TIMING CENSUS AND THE SPLIT JUDGE. Every CI report carries a non-gating timing section, and the two largest spec files are split by area without changing any test. Judge, frozen before any number existed: "the split is kept as a win if the median push→report wall time over the next three green CI runs is ≤ 19 minutes (80 % of the current ~24-minute median); if not, the timing section names the new long pole and nothing else changes without a new DEC." Rule recorded with it: the spec file is the shard unit, so no spec file carries more than about 25 tests per project; a file that would grow past that is split first. Rationale: `playwright.config.ts` sets `fullyParallel: false`, so a spec file is never split between shards and one large file is one shard's long pole; `post-wizard.spec.ts` (76 tests) and `admin-attributes.spec.ts` (64) were the two largest. Other levers (a bigger staging database, skipping browser tests on docs-only commits) wait until the timing section shows they are needed. Status (2026-10-04): built and landed (CI-T1, `7c300b6e`); the judge was MET on 2026-09-29 — the three green runs after the split took 14.3, 18.7 and 14.1 minutes from push to report (median 14.3); the slowest shard fell from about 20 minutes to 12.4.

THE PLACE STEP — W6, W6-R AND THE W6b DESIGN.
- Spec and approval. On the W4 walk the operator passed the place step with only the country chosen and ruled: "I THINK WE SHOULD FORCE SELECTION UP TO A CITY JUST LIKE WHAT WE DID WITH CATEGORY AND SHOULD BE RED IF NOT SELECTED … SUBCITY IS OPTIONAL", with additional places laid out as in the reference project apex. The supervisor's W6 spec was approved the same day ("AGREE W6"). Operator rulings carried by the prompt: R1 the item's location must be a city, a sub-city is optional, and the place heading and box wear the required mark and soft border until a city is chosen; R2 every place the listing is shown in is a city or a sub-city, and a region or a country alone never counts; R3 the prefill stays; R4 nested layout — a country box contains region boxes, a region box contains its cities, with the add buttons inside the box they extend; R5 the plan decides which add buttons appear (free plan: 1 city, 1 region, 1 country, so none).
- DEC-064 AMENDED (2026-09-29, operator "AGREE W6"). DEC-064 had allowed coverage at any level. Now every coverage place is a city or a sub-city; the door's `multipleMarkets` refusal is retired and countries are counted against the plan's `max_countries`; "everywhere" stays a later paid option.
- The executor's stop and the rulings on it. The executor stopped at the prompt's own stop rule with three findings, all confirmed by the supervisor's read: `submit_listing` calls `validate_listing_draft` with the step being saved, so places sent below step 6 were stored unjudged; the step-6 query wrote `v_lands` twice, so the country limit counted country-level places instead of countries; and the place step had never prefilled from the last listing. Rulings: (1) places are judged whenever they are sent, at any step, in the one door — never silently ignored, because dropping input silently is phantom success — while "coverage required" stays at step 6 and later (proof P27); (2) `v_lands` fixed in the same re-declaration, with a class scan for the same double write; (3) the last-listing prefill is not part of W6 (R3 corrected to "the existing prefill (saved-area cookie → location guess) stays"; S72). A read-only live count found 10 listings with a region-level place (6 drafts, 4 in screening, none published); no data was rewritten.
- W6 first landing (`0e4adb22`; migration `20260929201601_a3a572bf` → mark `20260929235900`, applied on prod): the door rule `cityRequired`, the nested layout, plan limits from the schema (`PLAN_CITIES` deleted), six new strings in English and Amharic ("Choose a city." and the layout labels); tests PR-17, PW-80, PW-81, PW-82 and a component test. The migration then failed on staging (INC-340) and CI was red (run 36626287215).
- W6-R, the repair — LANDED CLEAN (`d662a91f`; migration `20260929205914_13cb1b22` → marks `20260930001000` and `20260929235900`; Tier B plus migration). Proofs build their own scratch markets (INC-340); the door counts distinct cities, regions and countries (INC-341; proofs P22–P29); the description limit follows the door's 5,000 (INC-342; PR-18); PR-17 seeds its own scratch region, city and sub-city. The executor's first apply attempt failed on a JSON syntax error in its own code for picking free country codes; nothing was applied. On staging the operator applied only `13cb1b22`; `a3a572bf` is recorded there by mark only and must never be applied there. The executor's local DEC-023 suite on staging: 207 passed, 3 skipped, 0 failed, none flaky (17.9 minutes). Run 36630568746 was red before the staging apply and green on re-run; promoted. The operator's walk steps 1–6 passed and W6 was closed on 2026-09-29. Left for W6b: INC-343 and one unused translation string ("All the places must be in one market").
- The W6b design (operator, 2026-09-29). The country/region/city list is where the advertisement is shown; beside each listed place a choose-one marker says this is where the advertised item or service is (exactly one, a city or a sub-city); the map centres on that place with an optional exact pin; the pin is the item's spot, and a tick "this is also my shop/office" saves it to the seller's profile. The operator asked to be questioned for clarification; four multiple-choice questions were put, and the recorded answers are: the map only where it matters (it stays a category setting); the pin is the item or service spot and a tick saves the shop/office (also editable in Settings); the next post prefills the last post's places and marked item place, without the pin, with a one-tap "Use my shop location"; the saved shop/office is shown on the seller card and storefront, not on every post. Decided with it: the work is Tier A (a shop location on the profile is personal data: owner-only read and edit, included in export and deletion, rounded to about 500 m on public pages unless the seller chose "exact"); showing the shop/office to buyers becomes a requirement of the listing-page and storefront builds, which do not exist yet.
- After the W6 walk the operator asked for a separate city box under each region, "Add region" inside the country box and "Add city" inside the region box as the admin plan allows, a tick beside each city for the item location, and a map that opens centred on that place where a pin can be dropped and saved ("I opened the map and no opetion to drop a pin and save and appears slow … please understand apex very well for this"). The supervisor had the reference repo studied read-only (apex uses Leaflet with Mapbox map images fetched with a key from a small server function and OpenStreetMap as fallback; it draws the chosen city's outline, lets the seller tap to drop a draggable pin, search an address or use "my location", and confirm) and split W6b in three: W6b-1 the layout, the single "Item or service is here" tick and the prefill from the last post (Tier B, no door change, no migration; prompt issued 2026-09-29 on base `d662a91f`, with tests PW-83 and PW-84 in a new `e2e/post-wizard-where.spec.ts`); W6b-2 the map rebuilt the apex way; W6b-3 the shop/office location on the profile. W6b-1's rulings: the step is where the ad is shown (heading "Where should this ad be shown?"); a city box appears only after its region is chosen and stays at least 280 px wide at 360 px; exactly one tick, sent first in the coverage list because the door's item place is `p_coverage[1]`; any city box can be removed while another remains, and removing the ticked one moves the tick; prefill order for a new post — the seller's most recent other listing (read with an explicit `seller_id` filter), then the saved-area cookie, then the location guess. The map-service choice for W6b-2 (Mapbox as in apex, or the free OpenStreetMap servers) was put to the operator, who asked first what is best for performance and budget at scale and whether a fast free alternative exists; the answer and everything after it belong to the next block.

DEC-088 (2026-09-29) — A CONDITION THAT TESTS TWO ANSWERS AT ONCE (QUEUED ENGINE CHANGE). The catalogue engine's `visible_when` can test one field. Hiding net weight when the unit of sale is per kg (and volume when it is per litre) needs a condition on two answers; the curator found the case in Cycle 27 Batch 1 and keeps the list of leaves waiting on it in its change note. The number was given to queue that engine change; no specification was written under it. Status (2026-10-05): the number DEC-088 does not recur in any later record; the queue item it named was carried on under INC-381 (block S48, 2026-10-01; INC-381's entry names DEC-088 as the number first given to this engine change on 2026-09-29) and built in bundle 4 step 26 — door migration M6 `20261004212627_e44f20e5`, mark `20261005100000`, on ethio-staging (by 2026-10-04 ~22:15Z, CI preflight green) and on ethio-prod (operator, 2026-10-04 ~23:00Z, read-back `20261005100000`); screens in turn 9 (`2b55ed15`, 2026-10-04: `visible-when.ts`, `ConditionPair`/`conditionMet`, the link editor's "And when" line) with tests AT-65, AT-66, PW-152; CI on `2b55ed15` run 37244952955 SUCCESS and promoted. The curator's rows for the 10 Food leaves are still to be delivered (the "engine batch", the next curator prompt after bundle 4; the curator was idle and waiting for it at 2026-10-05 02:02Z).

DEC-089 (2026-09-29, PROPOSAL ONLY; operator "wE WILL DISCUSS THOSE LATER") — A COUNTRY-LOCALISED CATALOGUE THROUGH TWO ADDITIVE DATA LAYERS. Proposed, not decided: (1) per choice, "offered only in these countries" (an untagged choice is offered everywhere, so today's catalogue needs no change); (2) per question, "ask only in these countries" and "prefill differently by country". Principles: the country the item is posted in decides what is offered, not the seller's nationality; old listings never break; "Other" stays everywhere and typed answers are collected per country; there is always one list per question, never a copy per country; a tag means "markets where this is sold", because an Ethiopian mart abroad sells Ethiopian products. Already per country in the repo: every listing records its country; each country has its currency, unit system and order of top-level categories; a category can be hidden in chosen countries. To carry into the decision when it is taken: the ETB fallback at `pricing-data.ts:196` and the 11 fields whose help text names Ethiopia. Proposed build trigger: before the first market outside the Ethiopian and Eritrean community opens (Kenya or Somalia), or sooner if a walk finds a wrong default in a diaspora country. ESTIMATE: 4–7 executor prompts for both layers with tests, more if browse filters also need country scoping. Status (2026-10-04): proposal, parked by the operator; no later record under this number.

CATALOG CYCLE 27 — THE FULL-CATALOGUE REVIEW (curator; every file audited by the supervisor before import):
- Origin. The supervisor's first draft ("Study C27 — identity facts, producer lists, origins and order", 2026-09-29) covered only the leaves the operator had named and was withheld by him. The brief that replaced it was issued in turn 923 (2026-09-29, between the operator's 02:12 objection and his 02:15 directive): "Study C27 — FULL-CATALOGUE REVIEW (every root, every leaf, every linked attribute, every option)", with the twelve classes A–L, the six counts (a)–(f), the batch order "Batch 1 — Food & Beverages and Books … Then the ruled walk order: Vehicles → Electronics → Real Estate → Construction Material → Services → Home & Garden → Fashion → Babies & Kids → every remaining root", the base "categories_7 · definitions_3 · links_2, or later" and "Start with Batch 1's change note only — no files until it is approved" (see FILLED GAPS for the class texts). What the later record shows of its method: the catalogue is reviewed batch by batch, root by root; every leaf gets a row for each of twelve classes A–L; six mechanical counts are run on the base and on the curator's draft merged state — (a) fixed spec left open, (b) option offered under the wrong product, (c) order violations, (d) required beyond class H, (e) missing Amharic, (f) year with no floor; each batch arrives first as a review note without files and then, after rulings, as step-2 files built on fresh exports with expected previews and the INC-296 approval list. The twelve classes as written in the brief (t923): A known products (locked facts; yes/no in a "lock when available" column until W4b), B lasting identity facts that are not the listing title, C list membership, D list or free text, E question order, F origin, G relevance, H required rows ("only the two card rows, the identity model level (DEC-086), and identity names such as book_title" — the wording S68 corrected), I numbers, units and years, J conditions, K words (EN and AM), L gaps (full texts in FILLED GAPS).
- Batch 1 — Food & Beverages and Books. Review note 2026-09-29 on the base categories_7 · definitions_3 · links_2 (12 Food leaves and Books): counts before → after (a) 5 → 0, (b) 100 → 0, (c) 46 → 0, (d) 12 → 1, (e) 0 → 0, (f) 1 → 0. Findings: brands under the wrong product or unverifiable (an injera bakery under honey, butter and spices; Other Food offered all 46 brands); "Sheno kibe" mislabelled as vegetable ghee; unit and weight asked before the brand on 11 leaves; missing fields (book title, honey colour, butter origin, oil type, teff origin and others); units the market does not use (edible oil per litre, chicken per kg, yogurt per litre).
- The supervisor's eleven rulings on Batch 1 (2026-09-29): (1) order law E supersedes "unit at 1" (2026-09-21) and D48 — identity (type → brand → write-in) → specs → made-in → unit, weight, volume, pieces → condition band → seller statements; (2) class H corrected (S68): the operator's rule is at least two must-fill card questions, so card 3 stays where every seller knows the answer and buyers look for it (freshness; growing region at Coffee & Tea) and is cleared on six brand rows and two sealed rows; (3) 17 brands deleted, 28 sourced brands added, and a write-in `brand_name` (shown when the brand is Other) wherever no reliable list exists; (4) one home per product, six moves; (5) six new spec fields; (6) `organic_certified` leaves the Food root and is linked on 7 leaves, and fasting-friendly is unlinked at Spices; (7) "Packaged & Imported" renamed "Pantry & Packaged / የጓዳ ዕቃዎችና የታሸጉ ምግቦች" (slug kept); (8) `book_title` is required without being a card — the door enforces `is_required` on any link; (9) Books gain publisher, exam, faith, age, and grades KG–12 only; (10) `volume_ml` up to 20,000 mL; (11) brands ordered by market presence. Two engine items were set aside as not the curator's to solve: yes/no locks (W4b) and the two-answer condition (DEC-088).
- Batch 1 delivery and import (2026-09-29). The fresh exports were byte-identical to the base (W4 changed no catalogue row). Files: `c27-b1-categories.csv` (1 row), `c27-b1-definitions.csv` (35 rows, in dependency order), `c27-b1-links.csv` (1,438 rows), a change note, the form-path dispositions and the INC-296 list (10 new fields, 93 new options, the renamed category). The supervisor's audit approved them, with a stated limit: the operator's fresh exports were not attached to the supervisor, so it compared against a rebuilt copy missing 7 inherited link rows. Import: categories "0 added · 1 changed · 0 retired · 0 reactivated · 0 deleted · 0 unchanged · 0 refused"; the first attributes pass showed "41 added · 126 changed · 2 unlinked · 0 deleted · 1293 unchanged · 11 refused" and was discarded — one cause: the `brand-food` option `nan` carried the aliases "NAN" and "Nan", and the importer refuses a spelling that repeats case-insensitively anywhere in the definition (`aliasDuplicate`), which cascaded to ten rows naming the new brands (S74). With the corrected definitions file the pass read "41 added · 137 changed · 2 unlinked · 0 deleted · 1293 unchanged · 0 refused" — 180 changes written. Held in the curator's change note: the 12 yes/no facts to lock when the yes/no lock exists, and the 10 leaves waiting on two-answer conditions.
- Batch 2 — Fashion, Babies & Kids and the size and shoe rows of Sports Equipment (moved up on 2026-09-29 because these roots share the size, shoe-size, "For", brand and material lists). CLASS RULES FORGED: joining the parent set is not a move; only a changed answer resets (INC-329). The round-trip law is a standing test (PW-74), and a walk finding gets a test that is red before its fix; behaviour on big lists is tested on a list of more than 200 options. An own-row read filters by the signed-in user explicitly and never relies on RLS (INC-330). Places are judged whenever they are sent, in the one door; sent input is never silently dropped. G27 applies to migration proofs: a proof never depends on reference rows whose presence differs between projects, builds its own scratch rows inside a rolled-back block, and never skips with a notice (INC-340). A corrective migration may insert the superseded file's mark so the preflight accepts it without an allowlist line. A hook that purges drafts uses `stopPageBeforePurge` (INC-323). The spec file is the shard unit: about 25 tests per project per file (DEC-087). The flake rule is read from the running seven-day ledger, not from each run's flaky list (S69). Run-count judges are judged on CI runs, not on the executor's local runs. Curator audits repeat the importer's alias rules and carry the form-path counts in every batch; a definitions file is delivered in dependency order; a catalogue import is categories first, then definitions and links together.

DATES AND NAMES. Dates in this block are the operator's local dates (America/New_York, UTC−04:00); commits, CI runs and `docs/_changelog.md` lines are stamped in UTC, so work done after 20:00 local carries the next day's date there. The executor is Lovable; "the platform" is Lovable's own hosting, preview and commit machinery, which the executor does not control. "The door" is the database function that judges a posting step (`validate_listing_draft`, `validate_listing_attributes`, `set_listing_pin`). "The finder" is the server category search, `GET /api/catalog/find` backed by `catalog_find`. "The DEC-023 run" is the executor's local run, before it reports, of every `post-wizard-*` spec plus `posting-routes` and `a11y` on both Playwright projects (mobile-360 and desktop-1280). "Publish" is the platform's act of putting dev's code on the live site; catalogue imports are live without it. The W-series are the executor bundles built from the operator's wizard walks; W6 (the previous block) made every place a city.

MAP PROVIDER (decided 2026-09-30). The supervisor's first comparison (2026-09-29) recommended staying on free OpenStreetMap and moving to map data self-hosted on the operator's Cloudflare account (Protomaps, about $5–12 a month) before launch. The operator asked for a re-analysis on "best uer experience, lightweight and easy to use, secure not expensive"; the second pass added satellite view (sellers in Ethiopia find a place by landmarks, not street names) and Esri's free tier, and recommended Esri: ArcGIS Location Platform Static Basemap Tiles, streets and imagery, on the existing Leaflet library; free allowance as read from the pricing page 2,000,000 tiles a month, then $0.15 per 1,000 (ESTIMATE: 20–40 tiles per map session, so about 50,000–100,000 seller map sessions a month). Compared and not chosen: Mapbox, which the operator's apex app uses (street 200,000 free loads a month then $0.50 per 1,000; satellite 750,000 free then $0.25 per 1,000); MapTiler and Stadia (free plans do not allow business use); OpenFreeMap and Protomaps (no satellite, a heavier map library). Operator, 2026-09-30: "agree with your recomendation for asri, but we still need to have a backup built so if one doenst work we still have a fall baclk and a way to see on admin side if one is working and tracking cost". Built in W6b-2 with OpenStreetMap as the automatic fallback; self-hosting is reconsidered only if use passes about 1.5 million tiles a month for two months in a row. The apex app (`tesfayekb/apex-marketplace`, `src/components/maps/MapPinDropper.tsx`) is the reference for the pin experience only, not for its browser-side Nominatim calls or its token handling. Buyers do not load live maps by default (a map picture made once, or a "Show map" tap). The earlier promise to put "Cloudflare-hosted maps before launch" on the launch checklist lapsed with this decision; `docs/governance/launch-gate.md` carries no map line. Not confirmed: business use on Esri's free tier (the operator was asked to check the terms at sign-up).

DEC-090 — Not defined in the record; the number is not reused. (It falls between DEC-089 of 2026-09-29 and DEC-091 of 2026-09-30, where the only unnumbered decision is the map-provider choice of 2026-09-30; searched in every raw turn 835–1375, every sweep, the supervisor's notes, the uploads and the repository at dev `48d3c53b`.)

DEC-091 (2026-09-30, operator "approve both above") — ADMIN › SERVICES: EVERY OUTSIDE SERVICE IS TRACKED FOR HEALTH AND COST. One admin page with a row per outside service: a status light and last check; the error rate over the last 24 hours; usage this month against the free allowance; estimated cost against a budget the operator sets; which backup is in use. Services found in code: Supabase, Google Gemini (AI images, writing help), Google Translate, Nominatim (address lookup), OpenStreetMap, Esri, Telegram (sign-in); outside the code: Cloudflare, Lovable hosting, GitHub Actions, the email sender (the build confirms the full list). Health: a scheduled check every 15 minutes plus a success/failure count of every real server call. Usage and cost: exact for calls the server makes; estimated for map images, which phones fetch directly (Esri's own usage page stays the truth); hosting bills read from usage APIs where they exist, otherwise entered monthly; prices editable in admin. Alerts: an email to admins when a service is down, passes 80 % of its free allowance, or passes its budget. Backups: maps switch automatically (Esri → OpenStreetMap); the existing per-AI-feature on/off control moves to this page. Security: keys stay on the server; a services permission gates the page. Scope added the same day: API-key expiry dates, so admin warns before the Esri key runs out. Rationale: the operator's standing directive of 2026-09-30, "everyoutside resource we have /we use need to be tracked, like clusdeflare, supabase, the ai with all the costs and if those are working(a way to detect and need to have that interface inside admin". Status (2026-10-05): approved, not built — queued on 2026-09-30 after the flaky-test turn (CI-T2); no prompt was ever issued and no later mention exists through turn 1375 or in block S53; the map route's fallback log line (`src/routes/api/map/tiles.ts:113`) is written for it to count. The Esri API key created 2026-09-30 expires at most one year after creation; nothing warns before it does until this page exists.

DEC-092 (2026-09-30, supervisor; the operator did not override) — THE PLATFORM'S FRAMEWORK UPDATE STANDS ONLY IF CI IS FULLY GREEN ON IT. Inside a W6d turn the executor applied, unasked and reported as "a routine security update", @tanstack/react-router 1.170.16 → 1.170.41, @tanstack/react-start 1.168.26 → 1.168.60, @tanstack/router-plugin 1.168.18 → 1.168.42 and seroval 1.5.4 → 1.6.8, with a type fix in `src/routes/__root.tsx`. Asked for the advisory, it said the Lovable platform had named the packages and the exact versions and had given no advisory ID. The supervisor checked CVE-2026-45321 / GMS-2026-418 (react-start 1.167.68–1.167.71 only) and CVE-2026-59940 (seroval, fixed in 1.5.3); neither covered the previous versions. The serve stack is a closed surface (G22), so the update needed a named decision with a rule fixed before results: it stands only if CI on the first commit that carries it (`e765c012`) or its successor is fully green — every job, all six E2E shards, smoke, email and a11y, the first-paint bundle budget and the weight guard, with no new `[ssr-error]` lines; any red traced to it means reverting `package.json`, `bun.lock` and `src/routes/__root.tsx` to `07e6a523` in their own commit. Rejected: reverting at once for want of an advisory ID (it would put back versions the platform had flagged, to satisfy paperwork). Outcome: run 36781795077 red; AT-58 (the attributes-import route's 500 answer for a refused commit must carry its JSON message, INC-196 L2) proven red on the new versions and green with the three files at `07e6a523`, the same `[ssr-error] … commit_failed duplicate key value violates unique constraint "category_attribute_links_card_rank_unique"` line logged both times. REVERTED by its own rule in `5432ea61` (the first revert did not reach dev — INC-359). DEC-093 (2026-09-30, supervisor, flagged to the operator, who did not comment) — A VARIANT-SPLIT CELL COUNTS AS SETTLED FOR CATALOGUE COVERAGE. The curator works to a pre-committed bar: at least 95 % of a leaf's fact cells settled before its files ship (§8.3 of its rules). Buses & Vans stood at 94.9 % (74 of 78). The ruling, as sent to the curator: "a cell proven variant-split by two independent market sources, with no dominant variant, counts as settled for §8.3 coverage. Buses & Vans passes." Rationale: the Karry K60 is sold in Ethiopia with both gearboxes (Jiji: two manual 2022 petrol, one automatic 2025 electric; Cari: four automatics, one manual; the maker sells both), so no research can close the three open cells, and holding the leaf would block useful fixes (the coach seat locks, removing Offer Type). "K60 EV" is added as a model only when a battery and range source exists. Rejected: holding the leaf until fleet evidence shows a dominant version. Status (2026-10-04): in force; reused the same day for motorcycles (Boxer, HLX and Star as two-choice lists) and as the two-source bar for locking Yadea and Dodai to Electric (INC-358).

DEC-094 (2026-09-30, supervisor ruling on the operator's directive "are we going to make that message localized to ethiopia? … please put in knopwledge base") — MARKET-NEUTRAL WORDING AND THE `{country}` TOKEN. No UI string and no catalogue text (labels, options, helps) names Ethiopia or any one country where the statement depends on the seller's or buyer's market; such text is written with a `{country}` token that shows the market's country name in the screen's language. Product and cultural facts (teff, the Ethiopian calendar, a brand's name, cuisine) are not market statements and stay. Rationale: the Clothing walk showed the help "Sellers in Ethiopia choose Ethiopian-made." and the Amharic answer «እዚሁ የተሰራ» duplicating «የኢትዮጵያ ምርት»; texts tied to one country are hard to find and rewrite when a second market opens. Engine (executor Part T3): one resolver; the market comes from the draft's or listing's market, then the seller's detected market, then the fallback en "the seller's country" / am «የሻጩ ሀገር»; admin and exports show the raw token; the import gate accepts `{country}` and refuses any other `{…}` token; the finder indexes token-bearing text with the token removed. Line for `AGENTS.md` (Part T2), verbatim: "MARKET-NEUTRAL WORDING (DEC-094): UI strings and catalogue text (labels, options, helps) never name Ethiopia or any one country where the statement depends on the seller's or buyer's market; write it with the {country} token. Product and cultural facts (teff, the Ethiopian calendar, a brand's name) are not market statements." Curator rule from the same day: write no new market-specific text; convert existing rows only when told the token has landed. Rulings on the curator's census (`c27-dec094-market-wording-census-2026-09-30.csv`: 10 rows name the market, 4 are Ethiopia-only systems): (1) six neutral rewrites that need no token (voltage, `shoe_size_system`, `kibe_origin`, `net_weight_g`, honey "Yemen", `brand-salon` "Workshop-made (local)") done at once, in the Construction batch; (2) "Made in": `product_origin` survives and `origin-food` merges into it, with two answers, "Made in {country}" / «የ{country} ምርት» (value `local_made`) and "Imported"; this and the other token rows (`condition-vehicles` "Used in Ethiopia", `imei_registered`) wait for the token; (3) "Class B", Ethiopia-only systems that name no country (`plate_code`, `title_status`, `condo_scheme`, "libre" in the engine help), deferred until a second market opens, when a question will need to be shown only in certain countries. Later additions: country-dependent presets (220 V, EU shoe sizes, Wi-Fi-only tablets, electric cookers, locally made, rebar 12 m) and per-market units (lb, US gallons, cu ft, inches) wait for the same mechanism as per-market defaults. Status (2026-10-05): decided and BUILT in bundle 4 — door half in M6 `20261004212627_e44f20e5` (mark `20261005100000`, on ethio-prod and ethio-staging; `attr_cell_check` accepts `{country}` anywhere and refuses any other brace token except a valid `{category:<slug>}` in help text; `/api/translate` masks every brace token); screens in turn 9b, `0fc968e4` (2026-10-05 00:58Z): `src/i18n/catalog-tokens.ts` (catalogPieces / drawCatalog / catalogWords, `TOKEN_RE`), `src/features/posting/catalog-scope.ts` + `catalog-scope-provider.tsx` (tokenCountryCode: the ad's first place → the area cookie → the CONFIRMED home country → null), the wizard resolving the country name (open-market anchor name, else `Intl.DisplayNames`, else the fallback); tests PW-154/155/156 (`e2e/post-wizard-specs.spec.ts`), AT-67 (importer keeps tokens), TR-35 (translation keeps `{country}`), unit `catalog-tokens.test.ts`, `catalog-scope.test.ts`; `roadmap.md` "[x] Part G step 28 ({country}) screens — PW-154/155/156, AT-67, TR-35". As built the fallback key is `post.catalog.yourCountry` = "your country" / «አገርዎ» (`en.ts:2386`, `am.ts:2275`), not the decision's "the seller's country" / «የሻጩ ሀገር» wording. The `AGENTS.md` line (Part T2) has NOT landed at dev `48d3c53b` (no "MARKET-NEUTRAL WORDING" or "DEC-094" in `AGENTS.md`). Every curator conversion still waits: the token rows are in the "engine batch", the next curator prompt after bundle 4, not yet sent (2026-10-05 02:02Z). CI on `0fc968e4` (run 37249574367) was red on PW-129/PW-134 only, an unrelated identity-read race fixed in `24d401f5` (run 37251884569 SUCCESS, promoted).

The supervisor audits every delivery against its own merged model of the catalogue before the operator imports through Admin › Categories › Import and Admin › Attributes › Import (definitions and links together; the preview sums them), approves key names in Admin › Translations and walks the live site. PROPOSED, NOT INSTALLED (supervisor-instruction amendments offered to the operator; the installed instructions end at G28):
- G29 (proposed 2026-09-30 after S83): "every walk step states the answers that make its row appear, and its expected result is checked against the merged data and code before the walk is sent." DEC-095 (2026-10-01, supervisor ruling on the operator's Construction walk: "rather say go to X main, y submain for tractors etc") — POINTER HELP LINES NAME THE FULL PATH, THEN BECOME TAP-TO-GO LINKS. Step 1 (curator, at once): every help line that sends a seller to another category is a plain instruction naming the full path, every level, in English and Amharic — "Tractors go under Agriculture & Farming › Farm Equipment & Machinery." / «ትራክተር በግብርና እና እርሻ › የእርሻ መሳሪያዎች ሥር ይለጠፋል።»; such a line is exempt from the 60-character first-sentence rule; one destination per line, the rest behind the (i). Step 2 (executor, Part T4): catalogue help may carry `{category:<slug>}`; the same resolver as `{country}` renders it as the destination's full path in the screen's language, as a link; tapping it moves the draft to that leaf through the existing category-change path (the D59 reset with Undo); admin and exports show the raw token; the import gate accepts only slugs of active categories; the finder indexes the text with the token removed. Rationale: sellers can post only in the last level of a path, and "X: Root › Leaf." did not tell them what to do (searching «ኤሌክትሮኒክስ» lists Electronics' subcategories, not Electronics). Status (2026-10-04): step 1 done — all 57 pointer lines rewritten in the walk-fix batch, imported 2026-10-01, slugs listed in `c27-walk-pointer-lines-2026-10-01.csv` so the later swap is mechanical; step 2 BUILT as bundle 4 step 29 (help text only): migration M6 (`e44f20e5`) refuses a token whose slug is not an active category that accepts ads (`unknownCategoryToken`); the screens landed in turn 9b, `0fc968e4` (2026-10-05): `src/features/posting/catalog-words.tsx` draws the pointer as a button (`data-testid="post-category-pointer"`), `category-move-dialog.tsx` asks (`post.catalog.moveAsk` "Move this ad to {path}?" / «ይህ ማስታወቂያ ወደ {path} ይዛወር?», `moveKeeps`, `moveYes`), confirm re-reads the target's posting schema, keeps the answers whose key and type the target asks and moves the draft through `chooseLeaf`; a gone category draws nothing; in labels, options and units a pointer is never drawn; tests PW-157/158, AT-68 (unknown slug refused); `roadmap.md` "[x] Part G step 29 ({category:slug}, help text only) screens — PW-157/158, AT-68". DEC-097 (2026-10-01, supervisor; closed harness surface opened under G22 with a rule fixed in advance) — THE E2E ACCOUNT POOL. Supabase told the operator that organisation ethio had passed its plan's quota and that its Fair Use Policy applies from 2026-11-01. The usage pages showed 125,442 monthly active users on ethio-staging against 100,000 included, and 4 on ethio-prod; both projects sit in one organisation, so restrictions would hit production too. Cause: `e2e/helpers/users.ts` `createUser()` minted a fresh staging user for nearly every test (about 140 call sites) on every CI push, the nightly and every local run, about 4,000 a day, and a deleted user still counts for the month. Decision: (a) census every call site into (i) tests that need a brand-new identity, which keep minting, and (ii) tests that only need a signed-in account, which move to a pool; (b) the pool, staging only — fixed confirmed accounts `e2e-pool-<lane>-<NNN>@ethio-e2e.invalid`, never deleted by teardown; lanes never share accounts (one set per CI shard, one for the nightly, one for local runs); fresh random passwords each run through the admin API, kept in memory, so no new secret; a test leases one account per worker slot, reaped to a clean state before each lease; sized to the most concurrent leases per lane; (c) one line per run, "accounts signed in this run: <n> (pool <p>, fresh <f>)". Decision rule: ADOPT if three consecutive full green runs on the pool add no flake-ledger entry traceable to a shared account and each run's signed-in count is at most the pool size plus the (i) tests' count; otherwise REVERT to per-test minting. Rejected: turning the spend cap off and paying the overage (ESTIMATE about $55 a month and growing); the spend cap stays on. Census accepted with two rulings: the 11 auth-signout calls move to the pool wherever the test only needs a signed-in session; a pooled account gets the role its test asks for at lease, and tests that change a target account's status or roles mint that target fresh. As built: `leaseUser()`; lanes `s<shard>` on CI, `nightly` and `local`; seat = worker slot × 10 + the account's order within its test; accounts are created at first lease, not by global-setup, and roles are reset at the next lease, not at release (two deviations from the brief's wording, stated by the executor and now the documented design); still minting — mfa-stepup, auth-reset, auth-signin-errors, auth-callback, `@private-identity`, every admin-users target account and the "stranger" account of the category deny proof; AU-1 finds its admin's row by searching, because pooled accounts are old. Status (2026-10-04): built and on dev (`44e8eb85` after INC-376), PROVISIONAL. The first full run on the pool (36929256538) signed in 99 accounts (pool 25, fresh 74) and failed two tests in both projects (INC-377, INC-378), fixed in `b26bb0b3` (run 36931994260 green), with CI lanes then alternating by run-number parity (`s<shard>a` / `s<shard>b`). The decision rule was clarified (bring-up fixes for deterministic failures are allowed until the first fully green run) and then replaced by DEC-099 after a pool-traceable flake (INC-380): reset by census, the three-green count restarted at zero, the next pool-traceable red or flake reverts with no exception. No ADOPT statement is recorded through 2026-10-05: under DEC-099's restarted count two green runs were counted (`fb1063cb`, `34627301`), adoption was then held for a reset-timing line that was never built, and "test-account pool adoption" was named for the close-out bundle on 2026-10-03 (block S49); the pool is in use on every run (the report of run 37264070069 prints "accounts signed in this run: 5 (pool 2, fresh 3)" for the smoke source); `docs/features/e2e-harness.md` still reads "adopted provisionally 2026-10-01" at dev `48d3c53b` (line 339).

DEC-098 (2026-10-01, supervisor) — CI EVIDENCE MOVES OFF dev. Every bot commit — `ci-status.md`, `nightly-status.md`, `e2e-last-failure.md`, `guards-last-failure.md`, `nightly-last-failure.md`, `flake-ledger.md` — goes to its own branch, `ci-evidence`, and no workflow pushes to dev. Rationale: the platform cannot push an executor turn to dev when a report commit has moved dev during the turn, and pushes it to a `lovable-sync-<number>` branch instead; that happened four times in the record (INC-359 twice, INC-373, INC-376); the remote shows one earlier unrecorded stranding — `origin/lovable-sync-1790668701` (fork `425039fa`, 2026-09-29) holds `6b8e303a` "Work in progress" and `3b03ef30` "Added fake mode, updated refs" (07:58:13Z), the CI-T1 reference edits that never reached dev (block S45, CI-T1). Announced on 2026-10-01 at the fourth stranding and issued the same evening as "DEC-098 — CI evidence moves off dev (stage 1 of 2)", with the rule fixed in advance: ADOPT if the first two CI runs after landing each (a) put the four CI files on `ci-evidence` with `ci-status.md` naming that run's commit, (b) add no bot commit to dev and (c) still promote main on green; otherwise REVERT; the nightly's files are judged at the next 06:00 UTC nightly. Status (2026-10-04): stage 1 landed (`aeaf3d12`, run 36935469464 green, promoted; `scripts/publish-evidence.sh` with a self-test); both CI runs passed the rule on 2026-10-01 and the nightly's files reached the branch on 2026-10-02 ("fully adopted"). CI is read from `ci-evidence` since then; the copies on dev are frozen. Stage 2 — remove the six evidence files from dev with their paths-ignore and `.prettierignore` lines — is not done at dev `48d3c53b` (`roadmap.md` line 35: "awaits ADOPT"). How the executor reads the evidence changed on 2026-10-05: `git fetch origin ci-evidence` fails in its sandbox ("couldn't find remote ref" — its remote is its own mirror and carries only dev, main and its `lovable-backup-dev-*` branches), so the executor reads the three files at `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tesfayekb/ethio-marketplace/ci-evidence/docs/tracking/…` (the bundle 4 brief's line 13); `AGENTS.md:21` still gives the git form and is to be corrected in the records turn (supervisor slip S87: the turn 9b and turn 10 prompts said `git fetch origin ci-evidence`).

OPERATOR DIRECTIVES OF THE DAY (not numbered; his words):
- Curator prompts: "in future wait for the walk completeness rather than issuesing multiple prompts for curator before task completed". From then on the curator gets one message per batch, only after the walk passes.

SCOPE AND TERMS. This block covers the operator's evening of Thursday 2026-10-01 from 17:49 and the early morning of Friday 2026-10-02 to 07:50, America/New_York (ET, UTC−4). Dates are the operator's local dates; commit and CI stamps are UTC. "The pool" is the E2E account pool of DEC-097 (reused, leased staging accounts instead of one new account per test; defined in the preceding block). "D + L + M" are three parts of the executor's task list in `roadmap.md`: D, phone numbers refused in typed answers (refusal reason `contactInText`) and in the location note (`contactInNote`); L, the map zoom the seller chose saved with the pin (`pin_zoom`); M, the `other-food` catch-all correction. Other part labels used here: S1/S2 (category search timing, the index refresh and the single search call; target warm server p95 ≤ 300 ms), C (search never waits and makes one round trip), S3 (the "Searching…" row, INC-362), A (out-of-range numbers red as typed), B (prices say what they are per), Part O ("Other" write-ins, INC-369 and INC-370, with the shared readers of multi-choice answers), Part P (the place-step rework), Part T (market-neutral wording with a `{country}` token, and category links in help text), E census (posting-form load time). A "bundle" is several task-list items given to the executor in one brief. A "mark" is the row a migration writes into `public.migration_marks` as its last statement; "apply <fragment> → expect mark <value>" is the line with which the executor, who applies on ethio-prod itself, hands a migration to the operator for ethio-staging. "Red-first" means a new test is shown failing before its fix. "The DEC-023 run" is the executor's own local test run, required before it reports. "C27" is the curator's catalogue cycle 27, delivered in numbered batches; "the scan" is the curator's catalogue-wide list of selects settled to Other; "the root pass" is the review of all fifteen catalogue roots; "notes" are the non-blocking remarks of the catalogue audit.

DEC-099 (2026-10-01; no operator words recorded — the supervisor put the choice to him as "Pasting it is your approval. If you prefer the revert, tell me "revert the pool" instead", and he pasted the prompt) — THE ACCOUNT POOL RESETS BY CENSUS, NOT BY A HAND-WRITTEN LIST, AND ITS ADOPTION COUNT RESTARTS AT ZERO. One declared map in a pure-data module, `e2e/helpers/pool-reset-map.ts` (no imports): every place per-account state can live — every table in `src/integrations/supabase/types.ts` with a column holding a user id (the supervisor counted 19: attribute_import_revisions, audit_log, category_country_exclusions, category_import_revisions, country_root_order, coverage_plans, entity_translations, impersonation_sessions, languages, listing_revisions, listings, location_import_revisions, profiles, screening_verdicts, translator_languages, ui_translation_revisions, ui_translations, user_directory, user_roles — plus rate_limits, keyed by the id in `key`), the auth-side state (identities, factors, sessions, metadata, a pending email or phone change) and storage objects under a user's id — is either RESET (the reaper returns it to what `handle_new_user()` leaves) or EXEMPT with a one-line reason (attribution and history columns). `reapPoolAccount()` resets everything marked RESET; as built the reaper gained `translator_languages`, open impersonation sessions, `listing_revisions` and the e-mail re-set. A unit test under `src/` (`src/test/pool-reset-map.test.ts`; vitest includes `src/**` only, `vitest.config.ts:29`) reads `types.ts` (columns user_id, seller_id, actor_id, target_id, actor, reviewer, *_by) and fails when a table is in neither list. Class-wide test hygiene: a test undoes what it writes on a leased account. The reaper never catches and continues, never touches a real reference row, and the lane and seat logic is unchanged. Tier A (the helper holds the service role); no database change, no app code, no workflow file. Frozen rule, replacing DEC-097's count: "the count restarts at zero after this landing. Three consecutive green runs with no pool-traceable flake ADOPT. Any pool-traceable red or flake REVERTS the pool to per-test accounts, with no further bring-up." Rationale: INC-380 was the second occurrence of "the reset list is incomplete" (first: INC-377) and a pool-traceable flake after the first green run; DEC-097's frozen rule said REVERT, and the supervisor recommended against it because per-test accounts bring back about 4,000 new staging users a day (the supervisor's figure). This is a recorded exception to a pre-committed decision rule, made with the operator's approval, under a stricter replacement rule. Rejected: reverting the pool. Status (2026-10-04): built and landed (`fb1063cb`, run 36941645638 green, no flaky test = green run 1 of 3; guard test in the tree). Adoption OPEN: run 36944045541 on `34627301` was counted as green run 2 of 3, but adoption was held "until a timing line settles that question" (PW-98's cleanup timeout; the evidence does not show how long resets take), queued for after the D + L + M migration; no timing line, later count or ADOPT statement is recorded. On 2026-10-02 the staging transport failures (INC-385) were ruled not pool-traceable ("DEC-099's count is unchanged"). On 2026-10-03 "test-account pool adoption" was placed in the close-out bundle, and on 2026-10-04 the close-out bundle was restated as "slow-connection loading, search speed, one permission fix, older database warnings, the parked roadmap items, records". No ADOPT or REVERT statement exists through 2026-10-05; the pool is in use on every run (the report of run 37264070069 prints "accounts signed in this run: 5 (pool 2, fresh 3)" for the smoke source). `docs/features/e2e-harness.md` still reads "adopted provisionally 2026-10-01" at dev `48d3c53b` (line 339).

DEC-100 (2026-10-02, operator "keep sexual wellness as well as alcohol etc out.") — SEXUAL WELLNESS PRODUCTS ARE KEPT OUT OF THE CATALOGUE. They get no home and no search aliases, beside alcohol, tobacco and smoking accessories, which were already banned (REQ-028, DEC-060); prescription medicines stay banned (REQ-028). The supervisor also kept out, on the same census: software and digital goods, social-media accounts, businesses for sale, work-abroad agents, and an "on order" answer. Rationale: the market census counted 3,095 sexual-wellness ads and 507 alcohol ads on Jiji Ethiopia among the 5,822 listings with no home in the catalogue, and the curator asked whether to keep them out or give each a home. Stated limit: keeping a line out of the catalogue does not stop someone posting it under an "Other" leaf; blocking it at posting is the screening step's job, which is not built, and the list is recorded for it. The number DEC-100 was given by the supervisor in the reply to the operator and in the curator message of the same day. Status (2026-10-04): in force as a curator rule since 2026-10-02 (the census batch built nothing for these lines); not written in the repository until this record; the block at posting waits for the screening step. Last confirmed 2026-10-02.

SCOPE AND TERMS. This block covers Friday 2026-10-02 from 07:54 to Saturday 2026-10-03 15:09, America/New_York (ET, UTC−4). Dates are the operator's local dates; commit and CI stamps are UTC, so work done after 20:00 ET carries the next day's date in the repository. Terms of the preceding block apply. "Bundle 2" is the executor's brief saved at `docs/governance/briefs/bundle-2.md`: one migration, STEP 0 and 19 numbered steps in six parts — Part 0 (numbers owed from an earlier run), Part R (the phone rule), Part S (the search sweep), Part P (the place step), Part Q (contact and what a new post carries over), Part U (two small fixes). "The door" is a database function that validates and writes on the seller's behalf (`validate_listing_attributes`, `validate_listing_draft`, `set_listing_pin`). "The place step" is the posting wizard's step where the seller chooses the places the ad is shown in and marks one as where the item or service is; "the contact step" is the next one. "Alias" and "seller name" mean the same thing: the public name a seller posts under (REQ-008). "The wizard bundle" is the later posting-wizard bundle (bundle 4 after the renumbering of 2026-10-03). "U7" is the browse phase of the master plan, where the public listing page is built.

THE PHONE RULE, CLAUSES R1–R6 (INC-382; text of bundle 2 step 2, `attr_contact_like(text)`). A "run" is digits joined by at most two of these between digits: space, dot, hyphen, round bracket; a leading + belongs to the run. D is the run's digit count; a group is an unbroken string of digits. A text matches when: R1, a run starts with + and D is 8 or more; R2, a run's first digit is 0 and D is 9 to 15; R3, a group has 10 or more digits, or exactly 9 digits starting with 7 or 9; R4, D is 9, the first digit is 7 or 9, and every group has at least 2 digits; R5, the groups are 3-3-4, or 1-3-3-4 starting with 1; R6, the text holds an e-mail address, or a t.me/ or wa.me/ link. Nothing else matches. Must match (18): "+251 911 234 567" · "+251911234567" · "0911 23 45 67" · "0911234567" · "911234567" · "911 234 567" · "91 123 4567" · "09 11 23 45 67" · "251911234567" · "00251911234567" · "call 0911-234567" · "0911.23.45.67" · "(404) 555-1234" · "404.555.1234" · "+1 404 555 1234" · "abebe@example.com" · "t.me/abebe_shop" · "wa.me/251911234567". Must not match (16): "Sizes 42 43 44 45" · "Sizes 38 39 40 41 42 43" · "Sizes 90 92 94 96 98" · "Corolla 2008 1300cc" · "Bole Road, House 1234, 3rd floor" · "Model 320D" · "2015 2016 2017 models" · "was 150000 now 120000" · "150000000 birr" · "2023 12000 km" · "120 x 60 x 75 cm" · "Plot 25, Block 14, House 1234" · "1,500,000" · "ISBN 978-99944-0-000-0" · "Yeka, woreda 12, house 456" · "500 ETB per kg, minimum 10 kg". "The two lists are the judge": every row is an ASSERT in the migration and a case in the client mirror's unit test. Status: in force since 7423f49a. These R-numbers are a different series from the visibility plan's R1–R9 below.
